                digit_sum += left // right
                left, right = right, left % right
            total += 2 * digit_sum - 1
        costs[denominator] = total
    return costs


@njit(parallel=True)
def dyadic_cost(exponent: int) -> int:
    denominator = 1 << exponent
    total = 0
    for index in prange(denominator // 2):
        numerator = 2 * index + 1
        left, right = denominator, numerator
        digit_sum = 0
        while right:
            digit_sum += left // right
            left, right = right, left % right
        total += 2 * digit_sum - 1
    return total


def totients_through(limit: int) -> np.ndarray:
    values = np.arange(limit + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    values[1] = 1
    for prime in range(2, limit + 1):
        if values[prime] == prime:
            values[prime::prime] -= values[prime::prime] // prime
    return values


def finite_d_interval(costs: np.ndarray) -> Interval:
    lower = point(0)
    upper = point(0)
    for denominator in range(2, Q_CUTOFF + 1):
        lower = add(
            lower,
            mul(point(int(costs[denominator])), decimal_power(denominator, point(-SIGMA_HI))),
        )
        upper = add(
            upper,
            mul(point(int(costs[denominator])), decimal_power(denominator, point(-SIGMA_LO))),
        )
    return Interval(lower.lo, upper.hi)


def lower_d_tail(
    q_cutoff: int = Q_CUTOFF,
    p_cutoff: int = P_CUTOFF,
    sigma_upper: Decimal = SIGMA_HI,
) -> Interval:
    """Selected symmetric residues, integrated block by block."""
    phi = totients_through(p_cutoff)
    sigma = point(sigma_upper)
    total = point(0)
    for numerator in range(1, p_cutoff + 1):
        start = q_cutoff + numerator
        first = mul(
            div(point(4), mul(point(numerator), sub(sigma, point(2)))),
            decimal_power(start, sub(point(2), sigma)),
        )
        second = mul(
            div(point(6), sub(sigma, point(1))),
            decimal_power(start, sub(point(1), sigma)),
        )
        integral = sub(first, second)
        total = add(
            total,
            mul(rational(int(phi[numerator]), numerator), integral),
        )
    return total


def upper_d_tail() -> Interval:
    """Integral-test upper bound for q > Q_CUTOFF."""
    sigma = point(SIGMA_LO)
    delta = sub(sigma, point(2))
    log_q = decimal_ln(Decimal(Q_CUTOFF))
    q_one_minus_sigma = decimal_power(Q_CUTOFF, sub(point(1), sigma))
    polynomial = add(
        point(9), mul(point(8), integer_power(add(point(1), log_q), 3))
    )
    first_term = mul(q_one_minus_sigma, polynomial)

    exponential = decimal_power(Q_CUTOFF, neg(delta))
    integral_polynomial = point(0)
    coefficients = (17, 24, 24, 8)
    for degree, coefficient in enumerate(coefficients):
        degree_integral = point(0)
        for index in range(degree + 1):
            falling = factorial(degree) // factorial(degree - index)
            delta_power = integer_power(delta, index + 1)
            log_power = integer_power(log_q, degree - index)
            degree_integral = add(
                degree_integral,
                mul(point(falling), div(log_power, delta_power)),
            )
        integral_polynomial = add(
            integral_polynomial, mul(point(coefficient), degree_integral)
        )
    integral = mul(exponential, integral_polynomial)
    return add(first_term, integral)


def fraction_to_interval(value: Fraction) -> Interval:
    return rational(value.numerator, value.denominator)


def decimal_string(value: Decimal, places: int = 24) -> str:
    quantum = Decimal(1).scaleb(-places)
    with localcontext(CTX_NEAR):
        return str(value.quantize(quantum))


def dyadic_gamma_certificate() -> tuple[Fraction, Fraction, Fraction]:
    """Return the exact finite part, analytic tail, and resulting upper bound."""
    return GAMMA_25, GAMMA_TAIL, GAMMA_UPPER


def _regular_digit_sum(numerator: int, denominator: int) -> int:
    total = 0
    left, right = denominator, numerator
    while right:
        quotient, remainder = divmod(left, right)
        total += quotient
        left, right = right, remainder
    return total


def verify_complete_block_identity(max_denominator: int = 250) -> None:
    """Audit the exact block count and symmetric continued-fraction costs."""
    if max_denominator < 2:
        raise ValueError("max_denominator must be at least two")
    phi = totients_through(max_denominator)
    for modulus in range(1, max_denominator + 1):
        for block_index in range(3):
            start = block_index * modulus + 1
            coprime_count = sum(
                gcd(value, modulus) == 1
                for value in range(start, start + modulus)
            )
            if coprime_count != int(phi[modulus]):
                raise AssertionError(
                    f"complete-block identity failed for modulus {modulus}"
                )

    for denominator in range(3, max_denominator + 1):
        for numerator in range(1, (denominator + 1) // 2):
            if gcd(numerator, denominator) != 1:
                continue
            left = _regular_digit_sum(numerator, denominator)
            right = _regular_digit_sum(denominator - numerator, denominator)
            if left != right:
                raise AssertionError(
                    "symmetric cost identity failed for "
                    f"{numerator}/{denominator}"
                )
            if 4 * left - 2 < Fraction(4 * denominator, numerator) - 6:
                raise AssertionError(
                    f"symmetric cost lower bound failed for {numerator}/{denominator}"
                )


def _refined_root_interval(iterations: int = 18) -> Interval:
    lower = SIGMA_LO
    upper = SIGMA_HI
    for _ in range(iterations):
        midpoint = (lower + upper) / 2
        sign = ratio_minus_two(midpoint)
        if sign.lo > 0:
            lower = midpoint
        elif sign.hi < 0:
            upper = midpoint
        else:
            raise ArithmeticError("root sign interval contains zero")
    return Interval(lower, upper)


def build_certificate() -> SpeedCertificate:
    """Build a tighter certificate used by the regression-test interface."""
    sigma = _refined_root_interval()
    gamma_upper_i = fraction_to_interval(GAMMA_UPPER)
    v2_lower = div(decimal_ln(Decimal(2)), gamma_upper_i).lo
    h_upper = totient_dirichlet_log_moment(sigma.lo).hi

    extended_cutoff = 40_000
    costs = fixed_denominator_costs(extended_cutoff)
    d_finite = point(0)
    for denominator in range(2, extended_cutoff + 1):
        d_finite = add(
            d_finite,
            mul(
                point(int(costs[denominator])),
                decimal_power(denominator, point(-sigma.hi)),
            ),
        )
    d_tail = lower_d_tail(
        q_cutoff=extended_cutoff,
        p_cutoff=P_CUTOFF,
        sigma_upper=sigma.hi,
    )
    d_lower = CTX_DOWN.add(d_finite.lo, d_tail.lo)
    vc_upper = CTX_UP.divide(h_upper, d_lower)
    speed_gap = CTX_DOWN.subtract(v2_lower, vc_upper)
    return SpeedCertificate(
        sigma_lower=sigma.lo,
        sigma_upper=sigma.hi,
        gamma_upper=gamma_upper_i.hi,
        h_upper=h_upper,
        d_lower=d_lower,
        v2_lower=v2_lower,
        vc_upper=vc_upper,
        speed_gap_lower=speed_gap,
    )


def main() -> int:
    failures: list[str] = []

    dyadic_values = [dyadic_cost(exponent) for exponent in range(1, 26)]
    gamma_25 = sum(
        (Fraction(value, 3 ** (exponent + 1)) for exponent, value in enumerate(dyadic_values, 1)),
        Fraction(0),
    )
    if gamma_25 != GAMMA_25:
        failures.append("dyadic finite sum")

    exact_tail = sum(
        (
            Fraction(1, 3)
            * Fraction(2, 3) ** exponent
            * (
                Fraction(17, 2)
                + 16 * (1 + Fraction(7 * exponent, 20)) ** 2
            )
            for exponent in range(26, 500)
        ),
        Fraction(0),
    )
    # Close the geometrically weighted quadratic tail symbolically.
    ratio = Fraction(2, 3)
    start = 26
    sum_0 = ratio**start / (1 - ratio)
    sum_1 = ratio**start * (start - (start - 1) * ratio) / (1 - ratio) ** 2
    sum_2 = ratio**start * (
        start**2
        + (-2 * start**2 + 2 * start + 1) * ratio
        + (start - 1) ** 2 * ratio**2
    ) / (1 - ratio) ** 3
    closed_tail = Fraction(1, 3) * (
        Fraction(49, 25) * sum_2 + Fraction(56, 5) * sum_1 + Fraction(49, 2) * sum_0
    )
    if closed_tail != GAMMA_TAIL or GAMMA_25 + GAMMA_TAIL != GAMMA_UPPER:
        failures.append("dyadic rational tail")
    if exact_tail >= closed_tail:
        # The finite truncation must be strictly below the infinite closed tail.
        failures.append("dyadic tail sanity check")

    gamma_upper_i = fraction_to_interval(GAMMA_UPPER)
    log_two = decimal_ln(Decimal(2))
    v2_lower = div(log_two, gamma_upper_i).lo
    if v2_lower < V2_LO_CERT:
        failures.append("v2 lower endpoint")

    root_left = ratio_minus_two(SIGMA_LO)
    root_right = ratio_minus_two(SIGMA_HI)
    if root_left.lo <= 0 or root_right.hi >= 0:
        failures.append("critical root bracket")

    h_at_lo = totient_dirichlet_log_moment(SIGMA_LO)
    h_at_hi = totient_dirichlet_log_moment(SIGMA_HI)
    h_interval = Interval(h_at_hi.lo, h_at_lo.hi)
    if h_interval.lo < H_LO_CERT or h_interval.hi > H_HI_CERT:
        failures.append("critical H interval")

    costs = fixed_denominator_costs(Q_CUTOFF)
    finite_d = finite_d_interval(costs)
    if finite_d.lo < D_FINITE_LO_CERT or finite_d.hi > D_FINITE_HI_CERT:
        failures.append("finite D interval")

    lower_tail = lower_d_tail()
    upper_tail = upper_d_tail()
    if lower_tail.lo < D_TAIL_LO_CERT:
        failures.append("D lower tail")
    if upper_tail.hi > D_TAIL_HI_CERT:
        failures.append("D upper tail")

    d_interval = Interval(
        CTX_DOWN.add(D_FINITE_LO_CERT, D_TAIL_LO_CERT),
        CTX_UP.add(D_FINITE_HI_CERT, D_TAIL_HI_CERT),
    )
    if d_interval.lo != D_LO_CERT or d_interval.hi != D_HI_CERT:
        failures.append("stated D interval arithmetic")

    vc_upper = CTX_UP.divide(H_HI_CERT, D_LO_CERT)
    if vc_upper > VC_HI_CERT:
        failures.append("vc upper endpoint")
    separation = CTX_DOWN.subtract(V2_LO_CERT, VC_HI_CERT)
    if separation < SEPARATION_CERT or separation <= 0:
        failures.append("strict speed separation")

    print("STRICT SPEED SEPARATION VERIFICATION")
    print(f"dyadic_exact_exponents=1..25 gamma_25={GAMMA_25}")
    print(f"gamma_tail_upper={GAMMA_TAIL} gamma_upper={GAMMA_UPPER}")
    print(f"v2_lower={decimal_string(v2_lower, 24)}")
    print(
        "root_signs="
        f"[{root_left.lo},{root_left.hi}] "
        f"[{root_right.lo},{root_right.hi}]"
    )
    print(f"H_enclosure=[{h_interval.lo},{h_interval.hi}]")
    print(f"finite_D_enclosure=[{finite_d.lo},{finite_d.hi}]")
    print(f"tail_D_lower={lower_tail.lo} tail_D_upper={upper_tail.hi}")
    print(f"D_certificate=[{D_LO_CERT},{D_HI_CERT}]")
    print(f"vc_upper={decimal_string(vc_upper, 24)}")
    print(f"speed_gap_lower={decimal_string(separation, 24)}")
    print(f"failures={failures}")
    if failures:
        print("STATUS: FAIL")
        return 1
    print("STATUS: PASS")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Unit tests for the all-real pressure verification helpers."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import unittest

import verify_real_tilt_pressure as verification
from verify_real_tilt_pressure import (
    coexistence_local_counterexample_search,
    critical_slope_partial,
    matrix_bridge_audit,
    negative_continued_fraction_value,
    orbit_padding_counterexample_search,
    prime_support_inverse_h,
    prime_support_local_asymptotic_audit,
    prime_support_saddle,
    regular_partial_quotient_sum,
    stern_brocot_layer_denominators,
    stern_brocot_word_matrices,
)


class AllRealPressureHelpersTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_prime_support_inverse_and_projective_saddle_scaling(self) -> None:
        inverse = prime_support_inverse_h(2, 1.0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(inverse, 1.0 - 1.0 / math.sqrt(2.0), places=14)

        tau_unit, point_unit = prime_support_saddle((2, 3), (1.0, 1.0))
        tau_half, point_half = prime_support_saddle((2, 3), (0.5, 0.5))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(tau_half, 2.0 * tau_unit, places=13)
        for left, right in zip(point_unit, point_half):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(left, right, places=14)

    def test_prime_support_local_scale_matches_exact_diagonal_coefficients(
        self,
    ) -> None:
        audit = prime_support_local_asymptotic_audit(80)
        self.assertLess(abs(audit["corrected_ratio"] - 1.0), 0.01)
        self.assertGreater(audit["oracle_ratio"], 1.9)
        self.assertLess(audit["inverse_counterexample"], 0.5)
        self.assertGreater(audit["oracle_inverse"], 0.5)

    def test_single_layer_orbit_counter_recovers_partition_spectrum(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "single_layer_orbit_counter"))
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(18):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        generators = verification.weighted_generator_counters(16)
        partition_values = verification.ordinary_partition_values(
            fibonacci[18], fibonacci[2:18]
        )
        for layer in range(4, 16):
            predicted = verification.single_layer_orbit_counter(layer, generators)
            direct = verification.Counter(
                partition_values[
                    fibonacci[layer + 1] - 1 : fibonacci[layer + 2] - 1
                ]
            )
            self.assertEqual(predicted, direct)

    def test_critical_single_layer_renewal_matches_direct_partition_sum(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "critical_single_layer_partition"))
        sigma = 2.4787507857339603
        generators = verification.weighted_generator_counters(17)
        renewal = verification.critical_renewal_coefficients(generators, sigma)
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(20):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        partition_values = verification.ordinary_partition_values(
            fibonacci[19], fibonacci[2:19]
        )
        for layer in range(1, 17):
            predicted = verification.critical_single_layer_partition(layer, renewal)
            direct = sum(
                value ** (-sigma)
                for value in partition_values[
                    fibonacci[layer + 1] - 1 : fibonacci[layer + 2] - 1
                ]
            )
            self.assertAlmostEqual(predicted, direct, places=11)

    def test_finite_prime_support_interface_and_heavy_cost_obstruction(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(
            hasattr(verification, "prime_support_generator_cost_counter")
        )
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "heavy_dyadic_second_moment_terms"))

        dyadic = verification.prime_support_generator_cost_counter((2,), (6,))
        self.assertEqual(dyadic, verification.dyadic_generator_cost_counters(6)[6])

        mixed = verification.prime_support_generator_cost_counter((2, 3), (1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(sum(mixed.values()), 6)

        terms = verification.heavy_dyadic_second_moment_terms(20)
        self.assertEqual(len(terms), 20)
        self.assertTrue(all(right > left for left, right in zip(terms[2:], terms[3:])))
        self.assertGreater(terms[-1], 1000.0)

    def test_critical_window_renewal_matches_direct_partition_sum(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "critical_renewal_coefficients"))
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "critical_window_partition"))
        sigma = 2.4787507857339603
        generators = verification.weighted_generator_counters(17)
        renewal = verification.critical_renewal_coefficients(generators, sigma)

        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(20):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        coefficients = [1]
        for m in range(1, 17):
            coefficients = verification.extend_coefficients(coefficients, fibonacci[m])
            predicted = verification.critical_window_partition(m, renewal)
            direct = sum(level ** (-sigma) for level in coefficients)
            self.assertAlmostEqual(predicted, direct, places=11)

    def test_dyadic_exact_multiplier_counts_match_direct_fibers(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "dyadic_generator_cost_counters"))
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "dyadic_finite_window_count"))
        counters = verification.dyadic_generator_cost_counters(6)
        for exponent in range(1, 7):
            self.assertEqual(sum(counters[exponent].values()), 3 ** (exponent - 1))

        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(24):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        coefficients = [1]
        for m in range(1, 21):
            coefficients = verification.extend_coefficients(coefficients, fibonacci[m])
            direct = verification.Counter(coefficients)
            for exponent in range(2, 7):
                self.assertEqual(
                    verification.dyadic_finite_window_count(m, counters[exponent]),
                    direct[2**exponent],
                )

    def test_conditional_marked_scan_matches_direct_band_counts(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "conditional_marked_scan"))
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(20):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        generators = verification.weighted_generator_counters(18)
        rows = verification.conditional_marked_scan(
            (12, 16), generators, ((0.08, 0.02),)
        )
        coefficients = [1]
        direct_counts = {}
        for m in range(1, 17):
            coefficients = verification.extend_coefficients(coefficients, fibonacci[m])
            if m in (12, 16):
                direct_counts[m] = sum(
                    1
                    for level in coefficients
                    if abs(math.log(level) / m - 0.08) < 0.02
                )
        self.assertEqual([row[3] for row in rows], [direct_counts[12], direct_counts[16]])
        self.assertTrue(all(0.06 < row[4] < 0.10 for row in rows))
        self.assertTrue(all(0.0 < row[5] <= 1.0 for row in rows))

    def test_marked_window_counter_recovers_exact_fiber_spectrum(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "marked_window_counter"))
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(16):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        generators = verification.weighted_generator_counters(13)
        coefficients = [1]
        for m in range(1, 13):
            coefficients = verification.extend_coefficients(coefficients, fibonacci[m])
            if m < 4:
                continue
            marked = verification.marked_window_counter(m, generators)
            predicted = verification.Counter()
            for (cost, level), count in marked.items():
                self.assertLessEqual(cost, m + 1)
                predicted[level] += count
            actual = verification.Counter(coefficients)
            del actual[1]
            self.assertEqual(predicted, actual)

    def test_single_layer_joint_counter_recovers_exact_partition_spectrum(
        self,
    ) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "single_layer_joint_counter"))
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(16):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        generators = verification.weighted_generator_counters(13)
        partition_values = verification.ordinary_partition_values(
            fibonacci[16], fibonacci[2:16]
        )
        for layer in range(6, 13):
            joint = verification.single_layer_joint_counter(layer, generators)
            marginal = verification.Counter()
            for (cost, level), count in joint.items():
                if level <= 2:
                    self.assertEqual(cost, 0)
                else:
                    self.assertGreater(cost, 0)
                    self.assertLessEqual(cost, layer)
                marginal[level] += count
            direct = verification.Counter(
                partition_values[
                    fibonacci[layer + 1] - 1 : fibonacci[layer + 2] - 1
                ]
            )
            self.assertEqual(marginal, direct)

    def test_low_reward_fillers_realize_every_cost_from_six(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(verification, "low_reward_cost_filler"))
        for target_cost in range(6, 301):
            denominators = verification.low_reward_cost_filler(target_cost)
            self.assertIn(len(denominators), (1, 2))
            costs = [2 * denominator - 1 for denominator in denominators]
            self.assertEqual(sum(costs), target_cost)
            self.assertLessEqual(
                sum(math.log(denominator) for denominator in denominators),
                2.0 * math.log(target_cost),
            )

    def test_regular_and_negative_costs_agree(self) -> None:
        examples = {
            (1,): (1, 2),
            (2,): (1, 3),
            (1, 1): (2, 3),
            (1, 2): (3, 5),
        }
        for composition, fraction in examples.items():
            self.assertEqual(
                negative_continued_fraction_value(composition), fraction
            )
            numerator, denominator = fraction
            self.assertEqual(
                regular_partial_quotient_sum(numerator, denominator) - 1,
                sum(composition),
            )

    def test_layer_counts_and_denominator_extrema(self) -> None:
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(10):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        for depth in range(1, 9):
            denominators = stern_brocot_layer_denominators(depth)
            self.assertEqual(len(denominators), 2 ** (depth - 1))
            self.assertGreaterEqual(min(denominators), depth + 1)
            self.assertLessEqual(max(denominators), fibonacci[depth + 2])
            self.assertEqual(
                sum(denominators), 2 * 3 ** (depth - 1)
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                sorted(denominators),
                sorted(
                    sum(matrix)
                    for matrix in stern_brocot_word_matrices(depth - 1)[-1]
                ),
            )

    def test_stern_brocot_matrix_bridge_inequalities(self) -> None:
        checks, failures = matrix_bridge_audit(5)
        self.assertGreater(checks, 0)
        self.assertEqual(failures, 0)

    def test_truncated_critical_slope_is_finite_and_positive(self) -> None:
        numerator, denominator, slope = critical_slope_partial(
            2.4787507857339603, 100
        )
        self.assertTrue(math.isfinite(numerator))
        self.assertTrue(math.isfinite(denominator))
        self.assertTrue(math.isfinite(slope))
        self.assertGreater(numerator, 0.0)
        self.assertGreater(denominator, 0.0)
        self.assertGreater(slope, 0.0)

    def test_orbit_capacity_and_padding_have_no_small_counterexamples(self) -> None:
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(18):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        failures, capacity_checks, padding_checks = (
            orbit_padding_counterexample_search(12, 16, fibonacci)
        )
        self.assertEqual(failures, [])
        self.assertGreater(capacity_checks, 0)
        self.assertGreater(padding_checks, 0)

    def test_coexistence_local_critical_bound_has_no_small_counterexamples(
        self,
    ) -> None:
        fibonacci = [0, 1]
        for _ in range(22):
            fibonacci.append(fibonacci[-1] + fibonacci[-2])
        failures, checks, rows = coexistence_local_counterexample_search(
            (12, 16, 20),
            fibonacci,
            2.4787507857339603,
            ((0.04, 0.02), (0.08, 0.02), (0.12, 0.02)),
        )
        self.assertEqual(failures, [])
        self.assertEqual(checks, 9)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), checks)
        self.assertTrue(all(row[3] > 0 for row in rows))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regression tests for the strict speed-separation certificate."""

from fractions import Fraction

import verify_speed_separation as certificate


def test_dyadic_tail_certificate_matches_exact_rational_values() -> None:
    partial = sum(
        (
            Fraction(certificate.dyadic_cost(exponent), 3 ** (exponent + 1))
            for exponent in range(1, 26)
        ),
        Fraction(0),
    )
    tail = certificate.GAMMA_TAIL
    upper = certificate.GAMMA_UPPER
    assert partial == Fraction(13_180_988_392_373, 2_541_865_828_329)
    assert tail == Fraction(126_600_871_936, 2_541_865_828_329)
    assert upper == Fraction(4_435_863_088_103, 847_288_609_443)


def test_complete_block_cost_identity_on_a_nontrivial_range() -> None:
    certificate.verify_complete_block_identity(max_denominator=250)


def test_certified_speed_gap_exceeds_oracle_claim() -> None:
    assert certificate.main() == 0

\begin{theorem}[Local microcanonical spectrum]
\label{thm:local-fiber-spectrum}
For every \(m\ge1\),
\[
 C_m(1)=2.
\]
The two singleton fibers have residues
\(F_{m+1}-1\) and \(F_{m+2}-1\).  For every \(k\ge2\),
\[
 C_m(k)\le4\Psi(k),
 \qquad
 C_m(k)=4\Psi(k)\quad(m\ge2k).
\]
In particular, \(C_m(2)=4\) for \(m\ge4\).  Moreover, the locally finite
measures \(\nu_m\) converge vaguely on the discrete space \(\mathbb N\) to
\[
 \nu_\infty=2\delta_1+4\sum_{k\ge2}\Psi(k)\delta_k.
\]
Equivalently, the masses of every finite subset of \(\mathbb N\) eventually
stabilize.
\end{theorem}
\begin{theorem}[Exact negative-tilt spectral limit]
\label{thm:spectral-partition-function}
For every \(s>\sigma_0\),
\[
 \lim_{m\to\infty}S_{-s}(m)=\mathcal Z(s)<\infty.
\]
For an arbitrary real tilt \(t\), the exact dichotomy is
\[
 \begin{cases}
 S_t(m)\longrightarrow
 \displaystyle\frac{4}{2-\zeta(-t-1)/\zeta(-t)}-2,&t< -\sigma_0,\\[8pt]
 S_t(m)\longrightarrow+\infty,&t\ge-\sigma_0.
 \end{cases}
\]
Thus \(-\sigma_0\) is the convergence boundary for the unnormalized
partition functions, independently of whether it is the boundary of the
zero-pressure set.
\end{theorem}
\begin{theorem}[Critical Gibbs spectrum]
\label{thm:critical-gibbs-spectrum}
As \(s\downarrow\sigma_0\),
\[
 \mathcal Z(s)=\frac{4}{\kappa(s-\sigma_0)}+O(1).
\]
For \(s>\sigma_0\), define
\[
 Q_{m,s}(k):=\frac{C_m(k)k^{-s}}{S_{-s}(m)}.
\]
Then \(Q_{m,s}\) converges in total variation to the probability measure
\[
 Q_s(1)=\frac2{\mathcal Z(s)},
 \qquad
 Q_s(k)=\frac{4\Psi(k)k^{-s}}{\mathcal Z(s)}\quad(k\ge2).
\]
In particular,
\[
 Q_s(1)\sim\frac\kappa2(s-\sigma_0).
\]
If \(K\) has law \(Q_s\), then
\[
 \mathbb E_s[\log K]
 =\frac1{s-\sigma_0}+O(1),
 \qquad
 \operatorname{Var}_s(\log K)
 =\frac1{(s-\sigma_0)^2}+O(1).
\]
\end{theorem}
\begin{theorem}[Generating-orbit Tauberian law]
\label{thm:psi-tauberian}
As \(x\to\infty\),
\[
 \sum_{k\le x}\Psi(k)
 \sim\frac{x^{\sigma_0}}{\sigma_0\kappa},
 \qquad
 \sum_{k\le x}\frac{\Psi(k)}{k^{\sigma_0}}
 \sim\frac1\kappa\log x.
\]
Consequently,
\[
 \left(\frac4\kappa+o(1)\right)\log m
 \le S_{-\sigma_0}(m)
 \le\left(\frac{2\log\varphi}{\kappa}+o(1)\right)m.
\]
\end{theorem}
\begin{theorem}[Pressure bounds in the transition strip]
\label{thm:pressure-transition-strip}
For \(0<s<\sigma_0\),
\[
 \max\left\{0,\log\varphi-s\log\frac2\varphi\right\}
 \le\liminf_{m\to\infty}p_m(-s)
\]
and
\[
 \limsup_{m\to\infty}p_m(-s)
 \le\min\left\{\log\varphi,
       \frac{\sigma_0-s}{2}\log\varphi\right\}.
\]
In particular,
\[
 \liminf_{m\to\infty}p_m(t)>0\qquad(t>-\beta_*).
\]
Together with \Cref{thm:negative-temperature-freezing}, this confines any
boundary of the zero-pressure set to
\[
 [-\sigma_0,-\beta_*]
 =[-2.478750785733960\ldots,-2.270559453959664\ldots].
\]
The width of this candidate strip is
\[
 \sigma_0-\beta_*=0.208191331774296\ldots.
\]
\end{theorem}
\begin{theorem}[All-real pressure and the freezing corner]
\label{thm:exact-negative-pressure}
\label{thm:all-real-pressure}
For every \(s\in\mathbb R\), the radius of convergence of \(B_s\) is
\begin{equation}
 R_B(s)=\exp\{-\mathfrak P(s/2)/2\}.
 \label{eq:Bs-radius}
\end{equation}
Thus \(R_B(s)<1\) for \(s<2\), and \(R_B(s)=1\) for \(s\ge2\).
The nonnegative real roots of \(B_s(r)=1\) are classified as follows:
\[
\begin{array}{c|c}
s<\sigma_0 & \text{a unique root }r_s\in(0,R_B(s)),\\
s=\sigma_0 & \text{the unique root }r_s=1=R_B(s),\\
s>\sigma_0 & \text{no root in }[0,R_B(s)].
\end{array}
\]
The pressure exists for every real tilt and satisfies
\begin{equation}
 P(-s):=\lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log S_{-s}(m)
 =\begin{cases}
   -\log r_s,&s<\sigma_0,\\
   0,&s\ge\sigma_0.
  \end{cases}
 \label{eq:all-real-pressure}
\end{equation}
Equivalently, for \(t>-\sigma_0\), \(P(t)>0\) is the unique solution of
\begin{equation}
 \sum_{q=2}^{\infty}
 \sum_{\substack{1\le p<q\\(p,q)=1}}
 q^t e^{-P(t)c(p/q)}=1,
 \label{eq:exact-negative-pressure}
\end{equation}
whereas \(P(t)=0\) for \(t\le-\sigma_0\).  In particular,
\begin{equation}
 \{t\in\mathbb R:P(t)>0\}=(-\sigma_0,\infty).
 \label{eq:exact-zero-pressure-set}
\end{equation}

The restriction of \(P\) to \((-\sigma_0,\infty)\) is real analytic and
strictly convex.  At \(t_c=-\sigma_0\), it has the finite one-sided
derivatives
\begin{equation}
 P'_-(t_c)=0,
 \qquad
 P'_+(t_c)=
 \frac{\displaystyle
  \sum_{q\ge2}\sum_{\substack{1\le p<q\\(p,q)=1}}
       q^{-\sigma_0}\log q}
 {\displaystyle
  \sum_{q\ge2}\sum_{\substack{1\le p<q\\(p,q)=1}}
       c(p/q)q^{-\sigma_0}}>0.
 \label{eq:critical-pressure-slopes}
\end{equation}
Consequently, \(P\) is not differentiable at the freezing point.
\end{theorem}
\begin{theorem}[Full large deviations across the freezing corner]
\label{thm:full-fiber-ldp}
Let \(\mathbf X_m\) be uniformly distributed on \(X_m\), and set
\[
 \begin{gathered}
 y_m(x):=\frac1m\log d_m(x),\qquad
 Y_m:=y_m(\mathbf X_m),\\
 t_c:=-\sigma_0,\qquad v_c:=P'_+(t_c),
 \qquad \alpha_+:=\frac12\log\varphi.
 \end{gathered}
\]
Then \((Y_m)\) satisfies a full large deviation principle with speed \(m\)
and good rate function
\begin{equation}
 I(\alpha)=\sup_{t\in\mathbb R}
 \{t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi\}.
 \label{eq:full-fiber-ldp-rate}
\end{equation}
Its effective domain is \([0,\alpha_+]\).  On the coexistence interval,
\begin{equation}
 I(\alpha)=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha
 \qquad(0\le\alpha\le v_c).
 \label{eq:frozen-affine-rate}
\end{equation}
More precisely, for every \(0<\alpha<v_c\),
\begin{equation}
 \begin{aligned}
 &\lim_{\varepsilon\downarrow0}\liminf_{m\to\infty}
 \frac1m\log\Pr\{|Y_m-\alpha|<\varepsilon\}\\
 &\quad=
 \lim_{\varepsilon\downarrow0}\limsup_{m\to\infty}
 \frac1m\log\Pr\{|Y_m-\alpha|<\varepsilon\}\\
 &\quad=-\bigl(\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\bigr).
 \end{aligned}
 \label{eq:frozen-local-large-deviation}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}
\begin{theorem}[Critical finite-size and coexistence law]
\label{thm:critical-finite-window-gibbs}
Let
\[
 \mu_C=
 \sum_{q\ge2}\sum_{\substack{1\le p<q\\(p,q)=1}}
 c(p/q)q^{-\sigma_0}.
\]
Then
\begin{equation}
 S_{-\sigma_0}(m)\sim\frac{4m}{\mu_C}.
 \label{eq:critical-linear-finite-size}
\end{equation}
If \(\widehat{\mathbf X}_m\) has law \(\widehat Q_m\) and
\(U\) is uniform on \([0,1]\), then
\begin{equation}
 \left(
  \frac1m\log d_m(\widehat{\mathbf X}_m),
  \frac1m\ell_m(\widehat{\mathbf X}_m)
 \right)
 \xrightarrow[m\to\infty]{\mathrm d}(v_cU,U).
 \label{eq:critical-uniform-coexistence}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proposition}[Weighted generator renewal]
\label{prop:weighted-generator-renewal}
For \(j\ge1\), let
\[
 \Lambda_j(s):=\sum_{n\in\mathcal I_j}R(n)^{-s}.
\]
Then, for every real \(s\),
\begin{equation}
 \Lambda_j(s)
 =1+u_j(s)+2\sum_{\ell=1}^{j-1}u_\ell(s)
 =2\sum_{\ell=0}^{j-1}u_\ell(s)+u_j(s)-1.
 \label{eq:weighted-layer-renewal}
\end{equation}
Consequently, for every \(m\ge1\),
\begin{equation}
 S_{-s}(m)
 =4\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}u_j(s)+3u_m(s)+u_{m+1}(s)-2.
 \label{eq:weighted-window-renewal}
\end{equation}
Moreover, with formal power series
\[
 B_s(z):=\sum_{\ell\ge1}b_\ell(s)z^\ell,
 \qquad U_s(z):=\sum_{j\ge0}u_j(s)z^j,
\]
one has
\begin{equation}
 U_s(z)=\frac1{1-B_s(z)}.
 \label{eq:weighted-renewal-generating-function}
\end{equation}
\end{proposition}
\begin{proposition}[Finite-prime-support coefficient interface]
\label{prop:prime-support-interface}
Fix distinct primes \(\mathcal P=\{p_1,\ldots,p_d\}\).  For
\(\mathbf a=(a_1,\ldots,a_d)\in\mathbb N^d\setminus\{\mathbf0\}\), put
\[
 q_{\mathbf a}:=\prod_{i=1}^d p_i^{a_i},
 \qquad
 A_{\mathbf a}(u):=
 \sum_{\substack{1\le h<q_{\mathbf a}\\(h,q_{\mathbf a})=1}}
 u^{c(h/q_{\mathbf a})},
\]
and define the formal series
\[
 A_{\mathcal P}(\mathbf z,u)
 :=\sum_{\mathbf a\ne\mathbf0}
 A_{\mathbf a}(u)\mathbf z^{\mathbf a}.
\]
If
\[
 U_j(q_{\mathbf n})
 :=\card{\{g:\text{\(g\) is a Weinstein generator, }
 R(g)=q_{\mathbf n},\ L(g)=j\}},
\]
with the empty-word convention \(U_0(1)=1\), then
\begin{equation}
 \sum_{\mathbf n\in\mathbb N^d}\sum_{j\ge0}
 U_j(q_{\mathbf n})\mathbf z^{\mathbf n}u^j
 =\frac1{1-A_{\mathcal P}(\mathbf z,u)}.
 \label{eq:prime-support-joint-renewal}
\end{equation}
For \(q_{\mathbf n}>2\), the exact finite-window count is
\begin{equation}
 C_m(q_{\mathbf n})
 =\sum_{j\ge0}K_m(j)U_j(q_{\mathbf n}).
 \label{eq:prime-support-window-interface}
\end{equation}
After the cost mark is removed, the series is rational:
\begin{equation}
 \sum_{\mathbf n\in\mathbb N^d}
 \left(\sum_{j\ge0}U_j(q_{\mathbf n})\right)\mathbf z^{\mathbf n}
 =\frac1{\displaystyle
 2-\prod_{i=1}^d\frac{1-z_i}{1-p_i z_i}}.
 \label{eq:prime-support-unmarked-rational}
\end{equation}
\end{proposition}
\begin{theorem}[Directional quenched law on finite prime support]
\label{thm:prime-support-directional-quenched}
Retain the notation of \Cref{prop:prime-support-interface}, and fix
\(\boldsymbol\rho\in\mathbb S_{+}^{d-1}\).  Put
\[
 g_i(z):=\frac{1-z}{1-p_i z},
 \qquad
 h_i(z):=\frac{z g_i'(z)}{g_i(z)}
 =\frac{(p_i-1)z}{(1-z)(1-p_i z)}.
\]
There are unique \(\tau>0\) and
\(\mathbf z=\mathbf z(\boldsymbol\rho)\in
\prod_i(0,p_i^{-1})\) such that
\begin{equation}
 \prod_{i=1}^d g_i(z_i)=2,
 \qquad h_i(z_i)=\tau\rho_i\quad(1\le i\le d).
 \label{eq:prime-support-directional-saddle}
\end{equation}
More explicitly, \(z_i=\zeta_i(\tau\rho_i)\), where
\begin{equation}
 \zeta_i(x)=
 \frac{(p_i-1)+(p_i+1)x-
  \sqrt{((p_i-1)+(p_i+1)x)^2-4p_i x^2}}
 {2p_i x}.
 \label{eq:prime-support-correct-inverse}
\end{equation}
The point \(\mathbf z\) is the unique point of
\(A_{\mathcal P}(\mathbf w,1)=1\) in the closed polydisc
\(\{|w_i|\le z_i\}\).

Give every letter \((\mathbf a,h/q_{\mathbf a})\) the probability
\begin{equation}
 \Pr_{\mathbf z}\{(\mathbf A,C)=
  (\mathbf a,c(h/q_{\mathbf a}))\}=\mathbf z^{\mathbf a}.
 \label{eq:prime-support-directional-letter-law}
\end{equation}
This is a probability law, and
\[
 \mathbb E_{\mathbf z}\mathbf A=2\tau\boldsymbol\rho,
 \qquad m_C(\boldsymbol\rho):=\mathbb E_{\mathbf z}C<\infty.
\]
Define its directional velocity by
\begin{equation}
 v_{\mathcal P,\boldsymbol\rho}:=
 \frac{2\tau\sum_i\rho_i\log p_i}{m_C(\boldsymbol\rho)}.
 \label{eq:prime-support-directional-velocity}
\end{equation}

Let \(\mathbf n_N=N\boldsymbol\rho+O(1)\in\mathbb N^d\), and let
\(W_N\) be uniform among the nonempty free generator words of total exponent
\(\mathbf n_N\).  If \(R_N\) is its number of letters and \(T_N\) its
total cost, then
\begin{equation}
 \frac{R_N}{N}\xrightarrow{\Pr}\frac1{2\tau},
 \qquad
 \frac{T_N}{N}\xrightarrow{\Pr}\frac{m_C(\boldsymbol\rho)}{2\tau},
 \qquad
 \frac{\log q_{\mathbf n_N}}{T_N}
 \xrightarrow{\Pr}v_{\mathcal P,\boldsymbol\rho}.
 \label{eq:prime-support-directional-free-law}
\end{equation}

Finally, let \(m_N\to\infty\) be integers such that
\begin{equation}
 \frac{\log q_{\mathbf n_N}}{m_N}\longrightarrow
 \alpha\in[0,v_{\mathcal P,\boldsymbol\rho}).
 \label{eq:prime-support-inactive-budget}
\end{equation}
Then the exact level
\(\{x\in X_{m_N}:d_{m_N}(x)=q_{\mathbf n_N}\}\) is nonempty for all
sufficiently large \(N\),
\begin{equation}
 C_{m_N}(q_{\mathbf n_N})
 \sim4\card{\{\text{free words of exponent }\mathbf n_N\}},
 \label{eq:prime-support-inactive-count}
\end{equation}
and, under the uniform law on this level,
\begin{equation}
 \frac{L(g_{m_N})}{m_N}
 \xrightarrow{\Pr}
 \frac{\alpha}{v_{\mathcal P,\boldsymbol\rho}}.
 \label{eq:prime-support-inactive-window-law}
\end{equation}
The free-word convergences are uniform when \(\boldsymbol\rho\) ranges over a
compact subset of \(\mathbb S_{+}^{d-1}\) and
\(\|\mathbf n_N-N\boldsymbol\rho\|\) is bounded.  The finite-window
conclusions are uniform under the additional requirement that the budget
ratio remain bounded away from one.
\end{theorem}

\section{Finite-window fibers}\label{sec:preliminaries}

Throughout, the Fibonacci numbers are indexed by
\[
  F_0=0,
  \qquad
  F_1=F_2=1,
  \qquad
  F_{n+2}=F_{n+1}+F_n
  \qquad (n\ge 0).
\]

\subsection{Stable words and the residue model}

\begin{definition}[Stable words and visible values]
\label{def:stable-words}
For \(m\ge 0\), let
\[
  \Omega_m:=\{0,1\}^m,
  \qquad
  X_m:=\{x\in\Omega_m:\ x_ix_{i+1}=0\text{ for }1\le i<m\}.
\]
For \(x\in X_m\), define
\[
  V_m(x):=\sum_{j=1}^m x_jF_{j+1}.
\]
For \(\omega=\omega_1\cdots \omega_m\in\Omega_m\), define
\[
  N_m(\omega):=\sum_{j=1}^m \omega_jF_{j+1}.
\]
Thus \(\Omega_0=X_0=\{\varepsilon\}\), where \(\varepsilon\) is the empty word,
and \(V_0(\varepsilon)=N_0(\varepsilon)=0\).
\end{definition}

\begin{proposition}[Visible values are complete]
\label{prop:Vm-bijection}
For every \(m\ge 0\), the map
\[
  V_m\colon X_m\longrightarrow \{0,1,\dots,F_{m+2}-1\}
\]
is a bijection.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
The zero word maps to \(0\), so it remains to treat
\(n\in\{1,\dots,F_{m+2}-1\}\).  By Zeckendorf's theorem
\cite{Zeckendorf1972}, \(n\) has a unique
expansion as a sum of nonconsecutive Fibonacci numbers.  Because
\(n<F_{m+2}\), that expansion cannot use the summand \(F_{m+2}\) or any larger
Fibonacci number; therefore it uses only the weights \(F_2,\dots,F_{m+1}\),
and hence determines a unique word \(x\in X_m\) with \(V_m(x)=n\).
Conversely every \(x\in X_m\) is a stable expansion in those same weights, so
\(V_m(x)\in\{0,\dots,F_{m+2}-1\}\).  Thus \(V_m\) is bijective.
\end{proof}

\begin{definition}[Finite-window fold]
\label{def:fold-map}
For \(m\ge 0\), define
\[
  \Fold_m(\omega):=
  V_m^{-1}\bigl(N_m(\omega)\bmod F_{m+2}\bigr),
  \qquad \omega\in\Omega_m.
\]
For \(x\in X_m\), define the fiber multiplicity
\[
  d_m(x):=\card{\Fold_m^{-1}(x)}.
\]
In particular, \(\Fold_0(\varepsilon)=\varepsilon\) and
\(d_0(\varepsilon)=1\).
\end{definition}

\begin{proposition}[Single overflow]
\label{prop:single-overflow}
For every \(m\ge0\) and every \(\omega\in\Omega_m\),
\[
  N_m(\omega)\le \sum_{j=1}^m F_{j+1}=F_{m+3}-2<2F_{m+2}.
\]
Hence there exist unique integers \(b\in\{0,1\}\) and \(r\in[0,F_{m+2})\) such
that
\[
  N_m(\omega)=bF_{m+2}+r,
\]
and
\[
  \Fold_m(\omega)=V_m^{-1}(r).
\]
Equivalently, there is a unique overflow bit \(b_m(\omega)\in\{0,1\}\) such
that
\[
  N_m(\omega)=V_m(\Fold_m(\omega))+b_m(\omega)F_{m+2}.
\]
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
The Fibonacci identity
\(\sum_{j=1}^m F_{j+1}=F_{m+3}-2\) is standard, and
\(F_{m+3}-2<F_{m+3}-1<F_{m+3}=F_{m+2}+F_{m+1}<2F_{m+2}\).  The displayed
formula for \(\Fold_m\) is simply the definition.
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[Operational meaning of the fold]
\label{rem:prefix-normalization}
Because only one overflow can occur, the finite-window fold agrees with
computing the full Zeckendorf expansion of \(N_m(\omega)\) and deleting the
possible leading \(F_{m+2}\)-digit.
The residue definition is the useful one for exact fiber analysis.
\end{remark}

\begin{corollary}[Residue characterization of fibers]
\label{cor:residue-characterization}
For \(x\in X_m\), write \(r_x:=V_m(x)\).  Then
\[
  \Fold_m(\omega)=x
  \iff
  N_m(\omega)\equiv r_x \pmod{F_{m+2}}.
\]
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
By definition,
\(\Fold_m(\omega)=V_m^{-1}(N_m(\omega)\bmod F_{m+2})\), and \(V_m\) is
bijective by \Cref{prop:Vm-bijection}.
\end{proof}

\begin{table}[ht]
\centering
\caption{Notation used throughout the paper.  The letter \(q\) is reserved
for a nonnegative integer moment order, whereas \(t\) denotes a real tilt.}
\label{tab:notation}
\begin{tabular}{@{}ll@{}}
\toprule
Symbol & Meaning \\
\midrule
\(\Omega_m\), \(X_m\) & raw binary words and legal visible words \\
\(\Fold_m\) & residue-defined finite-window fold \\
\(d_m(x)\), \(d_m^\#(r)\) & fiber multiplicity, indexed by a word or residue \\
\(\widetilde d_m(n)\) & coefficient of \(\prod_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})\) \\
\(R(n)\), \(R^\dagger(n)\) & ordinary and indexed Fibonacci partition counts \\
\(S_q(m)\), \(S_t(m)\) & integer-order moment and real-tilt sum \\
\(T_q^\dagger(N)\) & indexed partition power sum below \(N\) \\
\(D_m\) & largest fiber multiplicity \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabular}
\end{table}

\begin{proposition}[No globally analytic compact-operator representation]
\label{prop:no-analytic-compact-representation}
There do not exist a complex Banach space \(\mathcal B\), a connected open
set \(U\subset\mathbb C\) containing \(\RR\), and an operator-norm analytic
map
\[
 z\longmapsto\mathcal L_z\in\mathcal K(\mathcal B)
 \qquad(z\in U)
\]
into the compact operators with the following properties.  For every real
\(t\), the operator \(\mathcal L_t\) has a positive algebraically simple
isolated eigenvalue \(r(t)\); for every \(t_0\in\RR\), this eigenvalue is
separated from the remainder of the spectrum by a positive gap uniformly for
\(t\) in some real neighborhood of \(t_0\); and the family represents the
finite-window sums in the exponential sense
\[
 \lim_{m\to\infty}\frac1m\log S_t(m)=\log r(t)
 \qquad(t\in\RR).
\]
\end{proposition}

---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

Claiming work is complete without verification is dishonesty, not efficiency.

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence â‰  evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter â‰  compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion â‰  excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
âœ… [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
âŒ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
âœ… Write â†’ Run (pass) â†’ Revert fix â†’ Run (MUST FAIL) â†’ Restore â†’ Run (pass)
âŒ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
âœ… [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
âŒ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
âœ… Re-read plan â†’ Create checklist â†’ Verify each â†’ Report gaps or completion
âŒ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
âœ… Agent reports success â†’ Check VCS diff â†’ Verify changes â†’ Report actual state
âŒ Trust agent report
```

## Why This Matters

From 24 failure memories:
- your human partner said "I don't believe you" - trust broken
- Undefined functions shipped - would crash
- Missing requirements shipped - incomplete features
- Time wasted on false completion â†’ redirect â†’ rework
- Violates: "Honesty is a core value. If you lie, you'll be replaced."

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

## The Bottom Line

**No shortcuts for verification.**

Run the command. Read the output. THEN claim the result.

This is non-negotiable.

+外来且已引用的还有 Feller 更新定理、Wiener--Ikehara、Gärtner--Ellis/Dembo--Zeitouni、解析扰动论及 Pemantle--Wilson 光滑极小点理论；Sanna 的整数次矩与零温端点、Kempton 的矩阵余循环动力学、Sarig 的可数状态相变仅作最近比较，均未被宣称为本文结果。
+本文自有部分是显式反转--补映射与仿射置换、分拆差及双区间恒等式、早层一致计数界、带代价更新恒等式及全实压力转移、临界线性常数与均匀共存律、补齐两种奇偶的非暴露斜率下界、二维 LDP，以及有限素数支撑的精确接口与严格非活跃区淬火转移；极值数值及极大整数本身不主张新颖。

+`verify_real_tilt_pressure.py` 在 (m=12,16,20,24,28,32)、(t=-8,-7.75,ldots,10) 上检查压力/LDP 形状，并完成层界 2--22、9011 项全实层审计、7938 项桥接、3396 项局部谱、210 项实倾斜更新、1815 项轨道补齐等检查，报告 0 numerical failures；两组回归测试合计 20 passed。
+这些有限计算、符号恒等式检查和区间证书仅用于发现反例及复核公式，不替代无限性解析证明。

开发早期的有限状态归一化 transducer、碰撞核及其 Perron/递推多项式链条因反例而撤回；主文及补编不再声称 (lambda_q) 的代数性、算术窗递推多项式、精确核证书、Galois 群或 Chebotarev 密度。
归属边界：外来且已引用的是 Zeckendorf 唯一表示；Kocábová--Masáková--Pelantová 的 (R) 极值与全部极大整数；Weinstein 的自由生成元幺半群、乘子乘法性、轨道分解、最终层稳定及 Dirichlet 级数；Kesseböhmer--Stratmann 与 Fiala--Kleban--Özlük 的 Stern--Brocot/Knauf 分母压力。
外来且已引用的还有 Feller 更新定理、Wiener--Ikehara、Gärtner--Ellis/Dembo--Zeitouni、解析扰动论及 Pemantle--Wilson 光滑极小点理论；Sanna 的整数次矩与零温端点、Kempton 的矩阵余循环动力学、Sarig 的可数状态相变仅作最近比较，均未被宣称为本文结果。
本文自有部分是显式反转--补映射与仿射置换、分拆差及双区间恒等式、早层一致计数界、带代价更新恒等式及全实压力转移、临界线性常数与均匀共存律、补齐两种奇偶的非暴露斜率下界、二维 LDP，以及有限素数支撑的精确接口与严格非活跃区淬火转移；极值数值及极大整数本身不主张新颖。
文献审计检索了 arXiv、Crossref、Google Scholar、Semantic Scholar、zbMATH Open 及可见引文链，未定位到上述一层/有限窗全实压力、临界共存或完整 LDP；但 MathSciNet 无订阅访问，部分接口曾返回 429，因此这只是可复核的检索证据，绝非穷尽性优先权证明。
机器证据：`verify_deepening_delta.py` 精确枚举 (m=0,ldots,28)（最大系数数组 832040），核验双区间恒等式、闭式、递推及全部极大类，结果为 0 failure、0 counterexample；`verify_speed_separation.py` 以有理数和定向舍入区间证成 (v_2-v_c≥0.001171960512764161)，STATUS: PASS。
`verify_real_tilt_pressure.py` 在 (m=12,16,20,24,28,32)、(t=-8,-7.75,ldots,10) 上检查压力/LDP 形状，并完成层界 2--22、9011 项全实层审计、7938 项桥接、3396 项局部谱、210 项实倾斜更新、1815 项轨道补齐等检查，报告 0 numerical failures；两组回归测试合计 20 passed。
这些有限计算、符号恒等式检查和区间证书仅用于发现反例及复核公式，不替代无限性解析证明。

+对既约 (p/qin(0,1)) 的规范负连分数 (1/(a_1-1/(a_2-⋯-1/a_r)))（(a_i≥2)），令 (c(p/q)=2∑_i(a_i-1)+1)；σ_0>2 由 ζ(σ_0-1)/ζ(σ_0)=2 唯一确定，(μ_C=∑c(p/q)q^{-σ_0})，(v_c=∑q^{-σ_0}log q/μ_C)，(α_+=	frac12logφ)。

+若 (N_m) 在 ℐ_m 上均匀，则 (m^{-1}log R(N_m)) 以速度 (m) 满足完全 LDP，良率函数 (I(α)=sup_{tinℝ}{tα-P(t)+logφ})，有效域 ([0,α_+])，且 (I(α)=logφ-σ_0α) 于 ([0,v_c])。
+联合变量 ((B_m^R,Y_m^R)=(m^{-1}ℓ_m^R(N_m),m^{-1}log R(N_m))) 亦满足完全 LDP；(Lambda(a,t)=max{0,a+P(t)}-logφ)，且 (J(β,α)=(1-β)logφ+βI(α/β))（(0<β≤1,0≤α≤βα_+)），(J(0,0)=logφ)，其余为 (+∞)；在 (0≤α≤βv_c) 上 (J=logφ-σ_0α)。
+（B--C，精确有限模型）对 (m≥0)，将 (ωin{0,1}^m) 的和 (∑ω_jF_{j+1}) 模 (F_{m+2}) 折叠到唯一合法 Zeckendorf 词，纤维大小记 (d_m)，按剩余类记 (d_m^#)；每一纤维均非空。
+对 (m≥1)、(0≤n<F_{m+2})，(π_m(n)≡F_{m+1}n+F_{m+1}-1pmod {F_{m+2}}) 是置换，且 (d_m^#(π_m(n))=[z^n]∏_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})=R^†(n)-R^†(n-F_{m+1}))，其中 (R^†(n)=R(n)+R(n-1))。
+对 (m≥0,0≤r<F_{m+2})，(d_m^#(r)=R(F_{m+2}+r))（(0≤r≤F_{m+1}-2)），而 (d_m^#(r)=R(r))（(F_{m+1}-1≤r<F_{m+2})）；故纤维表恰为两个相邻标准层的 (R) 值。
+令 (D_m=max d_m)，则 (D_0=D_1=1)，(D_{2k}=F_{k+2} (k≥0))，(D_{2k+1}=2F_{k+1} (k≥1))，并且 (D_m=D_{m-2}+D_{m-4} (m≥6))。
+为完整给出极大剩余类，令 (G_j=F_{j+1})；(I_{2k+1}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1)（(k) 偶）或 (G_kG_{k-2}+1)（(k) 奇），(I_{2k}=G_{k+2}G_{k-5}+G_3+1)（(k) 偶）或 (G_{k+1}G_{k-4}+G_3+1)（(k) 奇），(J_{2k}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1)（(k) 偶）或 (G_kG_{k-2}+1)（(k) 奇）。

+“全实轴解析且严格凸的压力”不是未完成目标而是错误命题：已证 (P=0) 于整个冻结半轴而 (P(0)=logφ)。固定素数支撑在活跃截断 (j=m+O(1)) 的锐局部渐近亦未证；二进方向代价已有无穷二阶矩且无正指数矩，所缺为指数条件下的格点稳定/半稳定局部更新定理。

+归属边界：外来且已引用的是 Zeckendorf 唯一表示；Kocábová--Masáková--Pelantová 的 (R) 极值与全部极大整数；Weinstein 的自由生成元幺半群、乘子乘法性、轨道分解、最终层稳定及 Dirichlet 级数；Kesseböhmer--Stratmann 与 Fiala--Kleban--Özlük 的 Stern--Brocot/Knauf 分母压力。
+外来且已引用的还有 Feller 更新定理、Wiener--Ikehara、Gärtner--Ellis/Dembo--Zeitouni、解析扰动论及 Pemantle--Wilson 光滑极小点理论；Sanna 的整数次矩与零温端点、Kempton 的矩阵余循环动力学、Sarig 的可数状态相变仅作最近比较，均未被宣称为本文结果。
+本文自有部分是显式反转--补映射与仿射置换、分拆差及双区间恒等式、早层一致计数界、带代价更新恒等式及全实压力转移、临界线性常数与均匀共存律、补齐两种奇偶的非暴露斜率下界、二维 LDP，以及有限素数支撑的精确接口与严格非活跃区淬火转移；极值数值及极大整数本身不主张新颖。

+尊敬的专家：请您以 Transactions of the American Mathematical Society（TAMS）审稿人的标准审阅本稿，而非为我们寻找新选题或提出“升档”构想。

+以下为请您据以裁决的完整主张边界；若正文与此摘要不一致，也请将该不一致列为审稿意见。
+基本对象：\(F_0=0,F_1=F_2=1,F_{j+2}=F_{j+1}+F_j\)，\(\varphi=(1+\sqrt5)/2\)；\(R(n)\) 为把两个单位项视为同一项的互异 Fibonacci 分拆数（\(n<0\) 时为 0），\(\mathcal I_m=[F_{m+1}-1,F_{m+2}-1)\)。
+对既约 \(p/q\in(0,1)\) 的规范负连分数 \(1/(a_1-1/(a_2-\cdots-1/a_r))\)（\(a_i\ge2\)），令 \(c(p/q)=2\sum_i(a_i-1)+1\)；\(\sigma_0>2\) 由 \(\zeta(\sigma_0-1)/\zeta(\sigma_0)=2\) 唯一确定，\(\mu_C=\sum c(p/q)q^{-\sigma_0}\)，\(v_c=\sum q^{-\sigma_0}\log q/\mu_C\)，\(\alpha_+=\tfrac12\log\varphi\)（各和遍历 \(q\ge2,1\le p<q,(p,q)=1\)）。
+（A，一层热力学）对每个 \(t\in\mathbb R\)，\(Z_m^R(t)=\sum_{n\in\mathcal I_m}R(n)^t\) 满足 \(m^{-1}\log Z_m^R(t)\to P(t)\)。
+对 \(s>\sigma_0\)，\(Z_m^R(-s)\to2/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-1\)；在 \(s=\sigma_0\) 时 \(Z_m^R(-\sigma_0)\sim2m/\mu_C\)。
+在临界 Gibbs 律 \(\Pr(n)\propto R(n)^{-\sigma_0}\) 下，若 \(\ell_m^R(n)\) 为 Weinstein 生成元层代价（\(R(n)\le2\) 时置 0），则 \((m^{-1}\log R(n),m^{-1}\ell_m^R(n))\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)，\(U\sim{\rm Unif}[0,1]\)。
+若 \(N_m\) 在 \(\mathcal I_m\) 上均匀，则 \(m^{-1}\log R(N_m)\) 以速度 \(m\) 满足完全 LDP，良率函数 \(I(\alpha)=\sup_{t\in\mathbb R}\{t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi\}\)，有效域 \([0,\alpha_+]\)，且 \(I(\alpha)=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\) 于 \([0,v_c]\)。
+联合变量 \((B_m^R,Y_m^R)=(m^{-1}\ell_m^R(N_m),m^{-1}\log R(N_m))\) 亦满足完全 LDP；\(\Lambda(a,t)=\max\{0,a+P(t)\}-\log\varphi\)，且 \(J(\beta,\alpha)=(1-\beta)\log\varphi+\beta I(\alpha/\beta)\)（\(0<\beta\le1,0\le\alpha\le\beta\alpha_+\)），\(J(0,0)=\log\varphi\)，其余为 \(+\infty\)；在 \(0\le\alpha\le\beta v_c\) 上 \(J=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\)。
+（B--C，精确有限模型）对 \(m\ge0\)，将 \(\omega\in\{0,1\}^m\) 的和 \(\sum\omega_jF_{j+1}\) 模 \(F_{m+2}\) 折叠到唯一合法 Zeckendorf 词，纤维大小记 \(d_m\)，按剩余类记 \(d_m^\#\)；每一纤维均非空。
+对 \(m\ge1\)、\(0\le n<F_{m+2}\)，\(\pi_m(n)\equiv F_{m+1}n+F_{m+1}-1\pmod {F_{m+2}}\) 是置换，且 \(d_m^\#(\pi_m(n))=[z^n]\prod_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})=R^\dagger(n)-R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1})\)，其中 \(R^\dagger(n)=R(n)+R(n-1)\)。
+对 \(m\ge0,0\le r<F_{m+2}\)，\(d_m^\#(r)=R(F_{m+2}+r)\)（\(0\le r\le F_{m+1}-2\)），而 \(d_m^\#(r)=R(r)\)（\(F_{m+1}-1\le r<F_{m+2}\)）；故纤维表恰为两个相邻标准层的 \(R\) 值。
+令 \(D_m=\max d_m\)，则 \(D_0=D_1=1\)，\(D_{2k}=F_{k+2}\ (k\ge0)\)，\(D_{2k+1}=2F_{k+1}\ (k\ge1)\)，并且 \(D_m=D_{m-2}+D_{m-4}\ (m\ge6)\)。
+为完整给出极大剩余类，令 \(G_j=F_{j+1}\)；\(I_{2k+1}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(I_{2k}=G_{k+2}G_{k-5}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_{k+1}G_{k-4}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(J_{2k}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇）。
+极大类为 \(\{I_{2k+1}-1,G_{2k}-1-I_{2k+1}\}\)（\(m=2k\ge10\)）及 \(\{I_{2k}-1,J_{2k}-1,G_{2k-1}-1-I_{2k},G_{2k-1}-1-J_{2k}\}\)（\(m=2k-1\ge13\)）。
+其余 \((m;\mathcal M_m)\) 为 \((0;\{0\}),(1;\{0,1\}),(2;\{0\}),(3;\{0,1,3\}),(4;\{0,3\}),(5;\{3\}),(6;\{3,8\}),(7;\{3,8,11,16\}),(8;\{8,16,24\}),(9;\{16,24,29,37\}),(11;\{37,63,71,79,105\})\)；\(m=10,12\) 适用上述偶数通式。
+（D--G，局部谱、压力与大偏差）令 \(C_m(k)=|\{x:d_m(x)=k\}|\)，\(\Psi(k)\) 为 Weinstein 的层值 \(k\) 生成轨道数；则 \(C_m(1)=2\)，且对 \(k\ge2\)，\(C_m(k)\le4\Psi(k)\)，并在 \(m\ge2k\) 时等号成立。
+若 \(\kappa=-\left.\frac d{ds}\frac{\zeta(s-1)}{\zeta(s)}\right|_{\sigma_0}\)，则 \(S_{-s}(m)=\sum_xd_m(x)^{-s}\to4/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-2\) 当且仅当 \(s>\sigma_0\)；相应层值 Gibbs 律全变差收敛，极限配分函数在 \(\sigma_0\) 的主部为 \(4/[\kappa(s-\sigma_0)]\)，且 \(\mathbb E\log K=(s-\sigma_0)^{-1}+O(1)\)、\({\rm Var}(\log K)=(s-\sigma_0)^{-2}+O(1)\)。
+\(\sum_{k\le x}\Psi(k)\sim x^{\sigma_0}/(\sigma_0\kappa)\)；对每个实 \(t\)，\(P(t)=\lim m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\) 存在，\(t\le-\sigma_0\) 时为 0，\(t>-\sigma_0\) 时为方程 \(\sum_{q\ge2}\sum_{(p,q)=1}q^te^{-P(t)c(p/q)}=1\) 的唯一正解，并在该开区间解析且严格凸。
+临界点 \(t_c=-\sigma_0\) 有 \(P'_-(t_c)=0,P'_+(t_c)=v_c>0\)；且 \(S_{-\sigma_0}(m)\sim4m/\mu_C\)，有限窗临界 Gibbs 律满足 \((m^{-1}\log d_m,m^{-1}\ell_m)\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)。
+因此不存在如下全局表示：在包含 \(\mathbb R\) 的连通复邻域上算子范数解析的紧算子族，其每个实参数均有正的、代数单的、孤立且局部一致谱隙的特征值 \(r(t)\)，同时 \(m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\to\log r(t)\)。
+若 \(X_m\) 在合法词集上均匀，则 \(m^{-1}\log d_m(X_m)\) 以速度 \(m\) 满足与上文相同的完全 LDP、有效域及率函数；特别地 \(0<\alpha<v_c\) 的缩小邻域概率指数恰为 \(-I(\alpha)\)。
+对任意固定互异素数集 \(\mathcal P=\{p_i\}_{i=1}^d\)，稿件还证明生成元“素指数--代价”级数为 \(1/(1-A_{\mathcal P}(\mathbf z,u))\)，去掉代价标记后为 \([2-\prod_i(1-z_i)/(1-p_i z_i)]^{-1}\)，并以边界权 \(4,3,1\) 精确转移到 \(C_m(k)\)。
+若 \(\boldsymbol\rho\in\mathbb S_{+}^{d-1}\)、\(\mathbf n_N=N\boldsymbol\rho+O(1)\)，唯一鞍点由 \(\prod_i(1-z_i)/(1-p_i z_i)=2\)、\((p_i-1)z_i/[(1-z_i)(1-p_i z_i)]=\tau\rho_i\) 确定；令 \(m_C=\mathbb EC\)、\(v_{\mathcal P,\rho}=2\tau\sum\rho_i\log p_i/m_C\)。若 \(\log q_{\mathbf n_N}/m_N\to\alpha<v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)，则该精确纤维层最终非空，计数渐近为自由词数的 4 倍，且其均匀律下 \(L(g)/m_N\to\alpha/v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)（概率收敛；紧方向集及严格非活跃预算上一致）。
+未证明或已撤回的内容：三个具名问题均未解决，即 Weinstein 的 \(F\)-primitive 数刻画、其凸包猜想 9.1、以及 2022 年三类部件数差猜想 3.3；现有工具分别缺少次序敏感最小值比较、全斜率支撑不等式及三次单位根消去。
+“全实轴解析且严格凸的压力”不是未完成目标而是错误命题：已证 \(P=0\) 于整个冻结半轴而 \(P(0)=\log\varphi\)。固定素数支撑在活跃截断 \(j=m+O(1)\) 的锐局部渐近亦未证；二进方向代价已有无穷二阶矩且无正指数矩，所缺为指数条件下的格点稳定/半稳定局部更新定理。
+本文也不声称临界配分和的 \(m^{3-\sigma_0}\) 二阶项、任意薄窗条件极限定理或第二/固定第 \(k\) 大纤维的闭式分类。
+开发早期的有限状态归一化 transducer、碰撞核及其 Perron/递推多项式链条因反例而撤回；主文及补编不再声称 \(\lambda_q\) 的代数性、算术窗递推多项式、精确核证书、Galois 群或 Chebotarev 密度。
+归属边界：外来且已引用的是 Zeckendorf 唯一表示；Kocábová--Masáková--Pelantová 的 \(R\) 极值与全部极大整数；Weinstein 的自由生成元幺半群、乘子乘法性、轨道分解、最终层稳定及 Dirichlet 级数；Kesseböhmer--Stratmann 与 Fiala--Kleban--Özlük 的 Stern--Brocot/Knauf 分母压力。
+外来且已引用的还有 Feller 更新定理、Wiener--Ikehara、Gärtner--Ellis/Dembo--Zeitouni、解析扰动论及 Pemantle--Wilson 光滑极小点理论；Sanna 的整数次矩与零温端点、Kempton 的矩阵余循环动力学、Sarig 的可数状态相变仅作最近比较，均未被宣称为本文结果。
+本文自有部分是显式反转--补映射与仿射置换、分拆差及双区间恒等式、早层一致计数界、带代价更新恒等式及全实压力转移、临界线性常数与均匀共存律、补齐两种奇偶的非暴露斜率下界、二维 LDP，以及有限素数支撑的精确接口与严格非活跃区淬火转移；极值数值及极大整数本身不主张新颖。
+文献审计检索了 arXiv、Crossref、Google Scholar、Semantic Scholar、zbMATH Open 及可见引文链，未定位到上述一层/有限窗全实压力、临界共存或完整 LDP；但 MathSciNet 无订阅访问，部分接口曾返回 429，因此这只是可复核的检索证据，绝非穷尽性优先权证明。
+机器证据：verify_deepening_delta.py 精确枚举 \(m=0,\ldots,28\)（最大系数数组 832040），核验双区间恒等式、闭式、递推及全部极大类，结果为 0 failure、0 counterexample；verify_speed_separation.py 以有理数和定向舍入区间证成 \(v_2-v_c\ge0.001171960512764161\)，STATUS: PASS。
+verify_real_tilt_pressure.py 在 \(m=12,16,20,24,28,32\)、\(t=-8,-7.75,\ldots,10\) 上检查压力/LDP 形状，并完成层界 2--22、9011 项全实层审计、7938 项桥接、3396 项局部谱、210 项实倾斜更新、1815 项轨道补齐等检查，报告 0 numerical failures；两组回归测试合计 20 passed。
+这些有限计算、符号恒等式检查和区间证书仅用于发现反例及复核公式，不替代无限性解析证明。
+优先权问题请置于四项判断之首：我们的一个姊妹稿刚被审稿人发现遗漏的既有结果，致使整篇文章的优先权叙述发生根本改变；请尤其主动追查而不要仅认可现有文献表。
+1. 数学是否正确？请逐一指出任何您无法跟随、认为有缺口或认为错误的陈述及具体证明步骤。
+2. 结果是否新，且上述优先权叙述是否准确？请给出最近的既有工作，判断我们是否正确、充分地归功于它；若某项实为已知推论或标准转移，请明确指出并说明应如何改写归属。
+3. 在不再假设可解决邻近具名公开问题的前提下，这一增量是否足以达到 TAMS 的发表门槛？若不足，请给出最合适的具体期刊，而非仅称“降档”。
+4. 若进入修改，您会要求哪些必要修改？请区分致命问题、必须大修与可选改进，并对每一项异议说明何种补充引理、证明、反例、文献比较、计算核验或措辞收缩足以消除该异议，以及完成后您的裁决是否会改变。
+我们宁愿现在收到理由充分的硬性拒绝，也不希望得到缺少核查的温和接受；请勿为鼓励作者而降低标准。
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

+尊敬的专家：请您以 Transactions of the American Mathematical Society（TAMS）审稿人的标准审阅本稿，而非为我们寻找新选题或提出“升档”构想。
+上一轮我们曾询问本邻域真正可达的机会；您的坦率结论是：以现有方法，附近没有任何具名公开问题可被解决。故本轮唯一问题是：现稿能否按其现有增量发表，以及 TAMS 审稿人会要求什么。
+以下为请您据以裁决的完整主张边界；若正文与此摘要不一致，也请将该不一致列为审稿意见。
+基本对象：\(F_0=0,F_1=F_2=1,F_{j+2}=F_{j+1}+F_j\)，\(\varphi=(1+\sqrt5)/2\)；\(R(n)\) 为把两个单位项视为同一项的互异 Fibonacci 分拆数（\(n<0\) 时为 0），\(\mathcal I_m=[F_{m+1}-1,F_{m+2}-1)\)。
+对既约 \(p/q\in(0,1)\) 的规范负连分数 \(1/(a_1-1/(a_2-\cdots-1/a_r))\)（\(a_i\ge2\)），令 \(c(p/q)=2\sum_i(a_i-1)+1\)；\(\sigma_0>2\) 由 \(\zeta(\sigma_0-1)/\zeta(\sigma_0)=2\) 唯一确定，\(\mu_C=\sum c(p/q)q^{-\sigma_0}\)，\(v_c=\sum q^{-\sigma_0}\log q/\mu_C\)，\(\alpha_+=\tfrac12\log\varphi\)（各和遍历 \(q\ge2,1\le p<q,(p,q)=1\)）。
+（A，一层热力学）对每个 \(t\in\mathbb R\)，\(Z_m^R(t)=\sum_{n\in\mathcal I_m}R(n)^t\) 满足 \(m^{-1}\log Z_m^R(t)\to P(t)\)。
+对 \(s>\sigma_0\)，\(Z_m^R(-s)\to2/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-1\)；在 \(s=\sigma_0\) 时 \(Z_m^R(-\sigma_0)\sim2m/\mu_C\)。
+在临界 Gibbs 律 \(\Pr(n)\propto R(n)^{-\sigma_0}\) 下，若 \(\ell_m^R(n)\) 为 Weinstein 生成元层代价（\(R(n)\le2\) 时置 0），则 \((m^{-1}\log R(n),m^{-1}\ell_m^R(n))\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)，\(U\sim{\rm Unif}[0,1]\)。
+若 \(N_m\) 在 \(\mathcal I_m\) 上均匀，则 \(m^{-1}\log R(N_m)\) 以速度 \(m\) 满足完全 LDP，良率函数 \(I(\alpha)=\sup_{t\in\mathbb R}\{t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi\}\)，有效域 \([0,\alpha_+]\)，且 \(I(\alpha)=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\) 于 \([0,v_c]\)。
+联合变量 \((B_m^R,Y_m^R)=(m^{-1}\ell_m^R(N_m),m^{-1}\log R(N_m))\) 亦满足完全 LDP；\(\Lambda(a,t)=\max\{0,a+P(t)\}-\log\varphi\)，且 \(J(\beta,\alpha)=(1-\beta)\log\varphi+\beta I(\alpha/\beta)\)（\(0<\beta\le1,0\le\alpha\le\beta\alpha_+\)），\(J(0,0)=\log\varphi\)，其余为 \(+\infty\)；在 \(0\le\alpha\le\beta v_c\) 上 \(J=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\)。
+（B--C，精确有限模型）对 \(m\ge0\)，将 \(\omega\in\{0,1\}^m\) 的和 \(\sum\omega_jF_{j+1}\) 模 \(F_{m+2}\) 折叠到唯一合法 Zeckendorf 词，纤维大小记 \(d_m\)，按剩余类记 \(d_m^\#\)；每一纤维均非空。
+对 \(m\ge1\)、\(0\le n<F_{m+2}\)，\(\pi_m(n)\equiv F_{m+1}n+F_{m+1}-1\pmod {F_{m+2}}\) 是置换，且 \(d_m^\#(\pi_m(n))=[z^n]\prod_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})=R^\dagger(n)-R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1})\)，其中 \(R^\dagger(n)=R(n)+R(n-1)\)。
+对 \(m\ge0,0\le r<F_{m+2}\)，\(d_m^\#(r)=R(F_{m+2}+r)\)（\(0\le r\le F_{m+1}-2\)），而 \(d_m^\#(r)=R(r)\)（\(F_{m+1}-1\le r<F_{m+2}\)）；故纤维表恰为两个相邻标准层的 \(R\) 值。
+令 \(D_m=\max d_m\)，则 \(D_0=D_1=1\)，\(D_{2k}=F_{k+2}\ (k\ge0)\)，\(D_{2k+1}=2F_{k+1}\ (k\ge1)\)，并且 \(D_m=D_{m-2}+D_{m-4}\ (m\ge6)\)。
+为完整给出极大剩余类，令 \(G_j=F_{j+1}\)；\(I_{2k+1}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(I_{2k}=G_{k+2}G_{k-5}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_{k+1}G_{k-4}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(J_{2k}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇）。
+极大类为 \(\{I_{2k+1}-1,G_{2k}-1-I_{2k+1}\}\)（\(m=2k\ge10\)）及 \(\{I_{2k}-1,J_{2k}-1,G_{2k-1}-1-I_{2k},G_{2k-1}-1-J_{2k}\}\)（\(m=2k-1\ge13\)）。
+其余 \((m;\mathcal M_m)\) 为 \((0;\{0\}),(1;\{0,1\}),(2;\{0\}),(3;\{0,1,3\}),(4;\{0,3\}),(5;\{3\}),(6;\{3,8\}),(7;\{3,8,11,16\}),(8;\{8,16,24\}),(9;\{16,24,29,37\}),(11;\{37,63,71,79,105\})\)；\(m=10,12\) 适用上述偶数通式。
+（D--G，局部谱、压力与大偏差）令 \(C_m(k)=|\{x:d_m(x)=k\}|\)，\(\Psi(k)\) 为 Weinstein 的层值 \(k\) 生成轨道数；则 \(C_m(1)=2\)，且对 \(k\ge2\)，\(C_m(k)\le4\Psi(k)\)，并在 \(m\ge2k\) 时等号成立。
+若 \(\kappa=-\left.\frac d{ds}\frac{\zeta(s-1)}{\zeta(s)}\right|_{\sigma_0}\)，则 \(S_{-s}(m)=\sum_xd_m(x)^{-s}\to4/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-2\) 当且仅当 \(s>\sigma_0\)；相应层值 Gibbs 律全变差收敛，极限配分函数在 \(\sigma_0\) 的主部为 \(4/[\kappa(s-\sigma_0)]\)，且 \(\mathbb E\log K=(s-\sigma_0)^{-1}+O(1)\)、\({\rm Var}(\log K)=(s-\sigma_0)^{-2}+O(1)\)。
+\(\sum_{k\le x}\Psi(k)\sim x^{\sigma_0}/(\sigma_0\kappa)\)；对每个实 \(t\)，\(P(t)=\lim m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\) 存在，\(t\le-\sigma_0\) 时为 0，\(t>-\sigma_0\) 时为方程 \(\sum_{q\ge2}\sum_{(p,q)=1}q^te^{-P(t)c(p/q)}=1\) 的唯一正解，并在该开区间解析且严格凸。
+临界点 \(t_c=-\sigma_0\) 有 \(P'_-(t_c)=0,P'_+(t_c)=v_c>0\)；且 \(S_{-\sigma_0}(m)\sim4m/\mu_C\)，有限窗临界 Gibbs 律满足 \((m^{-1}\log d_m,m^{-1}\ell_m)\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)。

+若 \(\boldsymbol\rho\in\mathbb S_{+}^{d-1}\)、\(\mathbf n_N=N\boldsymbol\rho+O(1)\)，唯一鞍点由 \(\prod_i(1-z_i)/(1-p_i z_i)=2\)、\((p_i-1)z_i/[(1-z_i)(1-p_i z_i)]=\tau\rho_i\) 确定；令 \(m_C=\mathbb EC\)、\(v_{\mathcal P,\rho}=2\tau\sum\rho_i\log p_i/m_C\)。若 \(\log q_{\mathbf n_N}/m_N\to\alpha<v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)，则该精确纤维层最终非空，计数渐近为自由词数的 4 倍，且其均匀律下 \(L(g)/m_N\to\alpha/v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)（概率收敛；紧方向集及严格非活跃预算上一致）。

+机器证据：verify_deepening_delta.py 精确枚举 \(m=0,\ldots,28\)（最大系数数组 832040），核验双区间恒等式、闭式、递推及全部极大类，结果为 0 failure、0 counterexample；verify_speed_separation.py 以有理数和定向舍入区间证成 \(v_2-v_c\ge0.001171960512764161\)，STATUS: PASS。
+verify_real_tilt_pressure.py 在 \(m=12,16,20,24,28,32\)、\(t=-8,-7.75,\ldots,10\) 上检查压力/LDP 形状，并完成层界 2--22、9011 项全实层审计、7938 项桥接、3396 项局部谱、210 项实倾斜更新、1815 项轨道补齐等检查，报告 0 numerical failures；两组回归测试合计 20 passed。
+这些有限计算、符号恒等式检查和区间证书仅用于发现反例及复核公式，不替代无限性解析证明。

尊敬的专家：请您以 Transactions of the American Mathematical Society（TAMS）审稿人的标准审阅本稿，而非为我们寻找新选题或提出“升档”构想。

（A，一层热力学）对每个 \(t\in\mathbb R\)，\(Z_m^R(t)=\sum_{n\in\mathcal I_m}R(n)^t\) 满足 \(m^{-1}\log Z_m^R(t)\to P(t)\)。

+尊敬的专家：请您以 Transactions of the American Mathematical Society（TAMS）审稿人的标准审阅本稿，而非为我们寻找新选题或提出“升档”构想。
+上一轮我们曾询问本邻域真正可达的机会；您的坦率结论是：以现有方法，附近没有任何具名公开问题可被解决。故本轮唯一问题是：现稿能否按其现有增量发表，以及 TAMS 审稿人会要求什么。
+以下为请您据以裁决的完整主张边界；若正文与此摘要不一致，也请将该不一致列为审稿意见。
+基本对象：\(F_0=0,F_1=F_2=1,F_{j+2}=F_{j+1}+F_j\)，\(\varphi=(1+\sqrt5)/2\)；\(R(n)\) 为把两个单位项视为同一项的互异 Fibonacci 分拆数（\(n<0\) 时为 0），\(\mathcal I_m=[F_{m+1}-1,F_{m+2}-1)\)。
+对既约 \(p/q\in(0,1)\) 的规范负连分数 \(1/(a_1-1/(a_2-\cdots-1/a_r))\)（\(a_i\ge2\)），令 \(c(p/q)=2\sum_i(a_i-1)+1\)；\(\sigma_0>2\) 由 \(\zeta(\sigma_0-1)/\zeta(\sigma_0)=2\) 唯一确定，\(\mu_C=\sum c(p/q)q^{-\sigma_0}\)，\(v_c=\sum q^{-\sigma_0}\log q/\mu_C\)，\(\alpha_+=\tfrac12\log\varphi\)（各和遍历 \(q\ge2,1\le p<q,(p,q)=1\)）。
+（A，一层热力学）对每个 \(t\in\mathbb R\)，\(Z_m^R(t)=\sum_{n\in\mathcal I_m}R(n)^t\) 满足 \(m^{-1}\log Z_m^R(t)\to P(t)\)。
+对 \(s>\sigma_0\)，\(Z_m^R(-s)\to2/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-1\)；在 \(s=\sigma_0\) 时 \(Z_m^R(-\sigma_0)\sim2m/\mu_C\)。
+在临界 Gibbs 律 \(\Pr(n)\propto R(n)^{-\sigma_0}\) 下，若 \(\ell_m^R(n)\) 为 Weinstein 生成元层代价（\(R(n)\le2\) 时置 0），则 \((m^{-1}\log R(n),m^{-1}\ell_m^R(n))\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)，\(U\sim{\rm Unif}[0,1]\)。
+若 \(N_m\) 在 \(\mathcal I_m\) 上均匀，则 \(m^{-1}\log R(N_m)\) 以速度 \(m\) 满足完全 LDP，良率函数 \(I(\alpha)=\sup_{t\in\mathbb R}\{t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi\}\)，有效域 \([0,\alpha_+]\)，且 \(I(\alpha)=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\) 于 \([0,v_c]\)。
+联合变量 \((B_m^R,Y_m^R)=(m^{-1}\ell_m^R(N_m),m^{-1}\log R(N_m))\) 亦满足完全 LDP；\(\Lambda(a,t)=\max\{0,a+P(t)\}-\log\varphi\)，且 \(J(\beta,\alpha)=(1-\beta)\log\varphi+\beta I(\alpha/\beta)\)（\(0<\beta\le1,0\le\alpha\le\beta\alpha_+\)），\(J(0,0)=\log\varphi\)，其余为 \(+\infty\)；在 \(0\le\alpha\le\beta v_c\) 上 \(J=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\)。
+（B--C，精确有限模型）对 \(m\ge0\)，将 \(\omega\in\{0,1\}^m\) 的和 \(\sum\omega_jF_{j+1}\) 模 \(F_{m+2}\) 折叠到唯一合法 Zeckendorf 词，纤维大小记 \(d_m\)，按剩余类记 \(d_m^\#\)；每一纤维均非空。
+对 \(m\ge1\)、\(0\le n<F_{m+2}\)，\(\pi_m(n)\equiv F_{m+1}n+F_{m+1}-1\pmod {F_{m+2}}\) 是置换，且 \(d_m^\#(\pi_m(n))=[z^n]\prod_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})=R^\dagger(n)-R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1})\)，其中 \(R^\dagger(n)=R(n)+R(n-1)\)。
+对 \(m\ge0,0\le r<F_{m+2}\)，\(d_m^\#(r)=R(F_{m+2}+r)\)（\(0\le r\le F_{m+1}-2\)），而 \(d_m^\#(r)=R(r)\)（\(F_{m+1}-1\le r<F_{m+2}\)）；故纤维表恰为两个相邻标准层的 \(R\) 值。
+令 \(D_m=\max d_m\)，则 \(D_0=D_1=1\)，\(D_{2k}=F_{k+2}\ (k\ge0)\)，\(D_{2k+1}=2F_{k+1}\ (k\ge1)\)，并且 \(D_m=D_{m-2}+D_{m-4}\ (m\ge6)\)。
+为完整给出极大剩余类，令 \(G_j=F_{j+1}\)；\(I_{2k+1}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(I_{2k}=G_{k+2}G_{k-5}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_{k+1}G_{k-4}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(J_{2k}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇）。
+极大类为 \(\{I_{2k+1}-1,G_{2k}-1-I_{2k+1}\}\)（\(m=2k\ge10\)）及 \(\{I_{2k}-1,J_{2k}-1,G_{2k-1}-1-I_{2k},G_{2k-1}-1-J_{2k}\}\)（\(m=2k-1\ge13\)）。
+其余 \((m;\mathcal M_m)\) 为 \((0;\{0\}),(1;\{0,1\}),(2;\{0\}),(3;\{0,1,3\}),(4;\{0,3\}),(5;\{3\}),(6;\{3,8\}),(7;\{3,8,11,16\}),(8;\{8,16,24\}),(9;\{16,24,29,37\}),(11;\{37,63,71,79,105\})\)；\(m=10,12\) 适用上述偶数通式。
+（D--G，局部谱、压力与大偏差）令 \(C_m(k)=|\{x:d_m(x)=k\}|\)，\(\Psi(k)\) 为 Weinstein 的层值 \(k\) 生成轨道数；则 \(C_m(1)=2\)，且对 \(k\ge2\)，\(C_m(k)\le4\Psi(k)\)，并在 \(m\ge2k\) 时等号成立。
+若 \(\kappa=-\left.\frac d{ds}\frac{\zeta(s-1)}{\zeta(s)}\right|_{\sigma_0}\)，则 \(S_{-s}(m)=\sum_xd_m(x)^{-s}\to4/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-2\) 当且仅当 \(s>\sigma_0\)；相应层值 Gibbs 律全变差收敛，极限配分函数在 \(\sigma_0\) 的主部为 \(4/[\kappa(s-\sigma_0)]\)，且 \(\mathbb E\log K=(s-\sigma_0)^{-1}+O(1)\)、\({\rm Var}(\log K)=(s-\sigma_0)^{-2}+O(1)\)。
+\(\sum_{k\le x}\Psi(k)\sim x^{\sigma_0}/(\sigma_0\kappa)\)；对每个实 \(t\)，\(P(t)=\lim m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\) 存在，\(t\le-\sigma_0\) 时为 0，\(t>-\sigma_0\) 时为方程 \(\sum_{q\ge2}\sum_{(p,q)=1}q^te^{-P(t)c(p/q)}=1\) 的唯一正解，并在该开区间解析且严格凸。
+临界点 \(t_c=-\sigma_0\) 有 \(P'_-(t_c)=0,P'_+(t_c)=v_c>0\)；且 \(S_{-\sigma_0}(m)\sim4m/\mu_C\)，有限窗临界 Gibbs 律满足 \((m^{-1}\log d_m,m^{-1}\ell_m)\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)。
+因此不存在如下全局表示：在包含 \(\mathbb R\) 的连通复邻域上算子范数解析的紧算子族，其每个实参数均有正的、代数单的、孤立且局部一致谱隙的特征值 \(r(t)\)，同时 \(m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\to\log r(t)\)。
+若 \(X_m\) 在合法词集上均匀，则 \(m^{-1}\log d_m(X_m)\) 以速度 \(m\) 满足与上文相同的完全 LDP、有效域及率函数；特别地 \(0<\alpha<v_c\) 的缩小邻域概率指数恰为 \(-I(\alpha)\)。
+对任意固定互异素数集 \(\mathcal P=\{p_i\}_{i=1}^d\)，稿件还证明生成元“素指数--代价”级数为 \(1/(1-A_{\mathcal P}(\mathbf z,u))\)，去掉代价标记后为 \([2-\prod_i(1-z_i)/(1-p_i z_i)]^{-1}\)，并以边界权 \(4,3,1\) 精确转移到 \(C_m(k)\)。
+若 \(\boldsymbol\rho\in\mathbb S_{+}^{d-1}\)、\(\mathbf n_N=N\boldsymbol\rho+O(1)\)，唯一鞍点由 \(\prod_i(1-z_i)/(1-p_i z_i)=2\)、\((p_i-1)z_i/[(1-z_i)(1-p_i z_i)]=\tau\rho_i\) 确定；令 \(m_C=\mathbb EC\)、\(v_{\mathcal P,\rho}=2\tau\sum\rho_i\log p_i/m_C\)。若 \(\log q_{\mathbf n_N}/m_N\to\alpha<v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)，则该精确纤维层最终非空，计数渐近为自由词数的 4 倍，且其均匀律下 \(L(g)/m_N\to\alpha/v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)（概率收敛；紧方向集及严格非活跃预算上一致）。
+未证明或已撤回的内容：三个具名问题均未解决，即 Weinstein 的 \(F\)-primitive 数刻画、其凸包猜想 9.1、以及 2022 年三类部件数差猜想 3.3；现有工具分别缺少次序敏感最小值比较、全斜率支撑不等式及三次单位根消去。
+“全实轴解析且严格凸的压力”不是未完成目标而是错误命题：已证 \(P=0\) 于整个冻结半轴而 \(P(0)=\log\varphi\)。固定素数支撑在活跃截断 \(j=m+O(1)\) 的锐局部渐近亦未证；二进方向代价已有无穷二阶矩且无正指数矩，所缺为指数条件下的格点稳定/半稳定局部更新定理。
+本文也不声称临界配分和的 \(m^{3-\sigma_0}\) 二阶项、任意薄窗条件极限定理或第二/固定第 \(k\) 大纤维的闭式分类。
+开发早期的有限状态归一化 transducer、碰撞核及其 Perron/递推多项式链条因反例而撤回；主文及补编不再声称 \(\lambda_q\) 的代数性、算术窗递推多项式、精确核证书、Galois 群或 Chebotarev 密度。
+归属边界：外来且已引用的是 Zeckendorf 唯一表示；Kocábová--Masáková--Pelantová 的 \(R\) 极值与全部极大整数；Weinstein 的自由生成元幺半群、乘子乘法性、轨道分解、最终层稳定及 Dirichlet 级数；Kesseböhmer--Stratmann 与 Fiala--Kleban--Özlük 的 Stern--Brocot/Knauf 分母压力。
+外来且已引用的还有 Feller 更新定理、Wiener--Ikehara、Gärtner--Ellis/Dembo--Zeitouni、解析扰动论及 Pemantle--Wilson 光滑极小点理论；Sanna 的整数次矩与零温端点、Kempton 的矩阵余循环动力学、Sarig 的可数状态相变仅作最近比较，均未被宣称为本文结果。
+本文自有部分是显式反转--补映射与仿射置换、分拆差及双区间恒等式、早层一致计数界、带代价更新恒等式及全实压力转移、临界线性常数与均匀共存律、补齐两种奇偶的非暴露斜率下界、二维 LDP，以及有限素数支撑的精确接口与严格非活跃区淬火转移；极值数值及极大整数本身不主张新颖。
+文献审计检索了 arXiv、Crossref、Google Scholar、Semantic Scholar、zbMATH Open 及可见引文链，未定位到上述一层/有限窗全实压力、临界共存或完整 LDP；但 MathSciNet 无订阅访问，部分接口曾返回 429，因此这只是可复核的检索证据，绝非穷尽性优先权证明。
+机器证据：verify_deepening_delta.py 精确枚举 \(m=0,\ldots,28\)（最大系数数组 832040），核验双区间恒等式、闭式、递推及全部极大类，结果为 0 failure、0 counterexample；verify_speed_separation.py 以有理数和定向舍入区间证成 \(v_2-v_c\ge0.001171960512764161\)，STATUS: PASS。
+verify_real_tilt_pressure.py 在 \(m=12,16,20,24,28,32\)、\(t=-8,-7.75,\ldots,10\) 上检查压力/LDP 形状，并完成层界 2--22、9011 项全实层审计、7938 项桥接、3396 项局部谱、210 项实倾斜更新、1815 项轨道补齐等检查，报告 0 numerical failures；两组回归测试合计 20 passed。
+这些有限计算、符号恒等式检查和区间证书仅用于发现反例及复核公式，不替代无限性解析证明。
+优先权问题请置于四项判断之首：我们的一个姊妹稿刚被审稿人发现遗漏的既有结果，致使整篇文章的优先权叙述发生根本改变；请尤其主动追查而不要仅认可现有文献表。
+1. 数学是否正确？请逐一指出任何您无法跟随、认为有缺口或认为错误的陈述及具体证明步骤。
+2. 结果是否新，且上述优先权叙述是否准确？请给出最近的既有工作，判断我们是否正确、充分地归功于它；若某项实为已知推论或标准转移，请明确指出并说明应如何改写归属。
+3. 在不再假设可解决邻近具名公开问题的前提下，这一增量是否足以达到 TAMS 的发表门槛？若不足，请给出最合适的具体期刊，而非仅称“降档”。
+4. 若进入修改，您会要求哪些必要修改？请区分致命问题、必须大修与可选改进，并对每一项异议说明何种补充引理、证明、反例、文献比较、计算核验或措辞收缩足以消除该异议，以及完成后您的裁决是否会改变。
+我们宁愿现在收到理由充分的硬性拒绝，也不希望得到缺少核查的温和接受；请勿为鼓励作者而降低标准。
+不要重复之前内容.不要重复其他人已经发表公开的推理过程(定理、推论、猜想、命题及证明), 可以使用其他人的结论并引用.不要中间过程结论.请使用学术化语言表达, 禁止口语.

+尊敬的专家：请您以 Transactions of the American Mathematical Society（TAMS）审稿人的标准审阅本稿，而非为我们寻找新选题或提出“升档”构想。
+上一轮我们曾询问本邻域真正可达的机会；您的坦率结论是：以现有方法，附近没有任何具名公开问题可被解决。故本轮唯一问题是：现稿能否按其现有增量发表，以及 TAMS 审稿人会要求什么。
+以下为请您据以裁决的完整主张边界；若正文与此摘要不一致，也请将该不一致列为审稿意见。
+基本对象：\(F_0=0,F_1=F_2=1,F_{j+2}=F_{j+1}+F_j\)，\(\varphi=(1+\sqrt5)/2\)；\(R(n)\) 为把两个单位项视为同一项的互异 Fibonacci 分拆数（\(n<0\) 时为 0），\(\mathcal I_m=[F_{m+1}-1,F_{m+2}-1)\)。
+对既约 \(p/q\in(0,1)\) 的规范负连分数 \(1/(a_1-1/(a_2-\cdots-1/a_r))\)（\(a_i\ge2\)），令 \(c(p/q)=2\sum_i(a_i-1)+1\)；\(\sigma_0>2\) 由 \(\zeta(\sigma_0-1)/\zeta(\sigma_0)=2\) 唯一确定，\(\mu_C=\sum c(p/q)q^{-\sigma_0}\)，\(v_c=\sum q^{-\sigma_0}\log q/\mu_C\)，\(\alpha_+=\tfrac12\log\varphi\)（各和遍历 \(q\ge2,1\le p<q,(p,q)=1\)）。
+（A，一层热力学）对每个 \(t\in\mathbb R\)，\(Z_m^R(t)=\sum_{n\in\mathcal I_m}R(n)^t\) 满足 \(m^{-1}\log Z_m^R(t)\to P(t)\)。
+对 \(s>\sigma_0\)，\(Z_m^R(-s)\to2/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-1\)；在 \(s=\sigma_0\) 时 \(Z_m^R(-\sigma_0)\sim2m/\mu_C\)。
+在临界 Gibbs 律 \(\Pr(n)\propto R(n)^{-\sigma_0}\) 下，若 \(\ell_m^R(n)\) 为 Weinstein 生成元层代价（\(R(n)\le2\) 时置 0），则 \((m^{-1}\log R(n),m^{-1}\ell_m^R(n))\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)，\(U\sim{\rm Unif}[0,1]\)。
+若 \(N_m\) 在 \(\mathcal I_m\) 上均匀，则 \(m^{-1}\log R(N_m)\) 以速度 \(m\) 满足完全 LDP，良率函数 \(I(\alpha)=\sup_{t\in\mathbb R}\{t\alpha-P(t)+\log\varphi\}\)，有效域 \([0,\alpha_+]\)，且 \(I(\alpha)=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\) 于 \([0,v_c]\)。
+联合变量 \((B_m^R,Y_m^R)=(m^{-1}\ell_m^R(N_m),m^{-1}\log R(N_m))\) 亦满足完全 LDP；\(\Lambda(a,t)=\max\{0,a+P(t)\}-\log\varphi\)，且 \(J(\beta,\alpha)=(1-\beta)\log\varphi+\beta I(\alpha/\beta)\)（\(0<\beta\le1,0\le\alpha\le\beta\alpha_+\)），\(J(0,0)=\log\varphi\)，其余为 \(+\infty\)；在 \(0\le\alpha\le\beta v_c\) 上 \(J=\log\varphi-\sigma_0\alpha\)。
+（B--C，精确有限模型）对 \(m\ge0\)，将 \(\omega\in\{0,1\}^m\) 的和 \(\sum\omega_jF_{j+1}\) 模 \(F_{m+2}\) 折叠到唯一合法 Zeckendorf 词，纤维大小记 \(d_m\)，按剩余类记 \(d_m^\#\)；每一纤维均非空。
+对 \(m\ge1\)、\(0\le n<F_{m+2}\)，\(\pi_m(n)\equiv F_{m+1}n+F_{m+1}-1\pmod {F_{m+2}}\) 是置换，且 \(d_m^\#(\pi_m(n))=[z^n]\prod_{j=1}^m(1+z^{F_j})=R^\dagger(n)-R^\dagger(n-F_{m+1})\)，其中 \(R^\dagger(n)=R(n)+R(n-1)\)。
+对 \(m\ge0,0\le r<F_{m+2}\)，\(d_m^\#(r)=R(F_{m+2}+r)\)（\(0\le r\le F_{m+1}-2\)），而 \(d_m^\#(r)=R(r)\)（\(F_{m+1}-1\le r<F_{m+2}\)）；故纤维表恰为两个相邻标准层的 \(R\) 值。
+令 \(D_m=\max d_m\)，则 \(D_0=D_1=1\)，\(D_{2k}=F_{k+2}\ (k\ge0)\)，\(D_{2k+1}=2F_{k+1}\ (k\ge1)\)，并且 \(D_m=D_{m-2}+D_{m-4}\ (m\ge6)\)。
+为完整给出极大剩余类，令 \(G_j=F_{j+1}\)；\(I_{2k+1}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(I_{2k}=G_{k+2}G_{k-5}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_{k+1}G_{k-4}+G_3+1\)（\(k\) 奇），\(J_{2k}=G_{k+1}G_{k-3}+1\)（\(k\) 偶）或 \(G_kG_{k-2}+1\)（\(k\) 奇）。
+极大类为 \(\{I_{2k+1}-1,G_{2k}-1-I_{2k+1}\}\)（\(m=2k\ge10\)）及 \(\{I_{2k}-1,J_{2k}-1,G_{2k-1}-1-I_{2k},G_{2k-1}-1-J_{2k}\}\)（\(m=2k-1\ge13\)）。
+其余 \((m;\mathcal M_m)\) 为 \((0;\{0\}),(1;\{0,1\}),(2;\{0\}),(3;\{0,1,3\}),(4;\{0,3\}),(5;\{3\}),(6;\{3,8\}),(7;\{3,8,11,16\}),(8;\{8,16,24\}),(9;\{16,24,29,37\}),(11;\{37,63,71,79,105\})\)；\(m=10,12\) 适用上述偶数通式。
+（D--G，局部谱、压力与大偏差）令 \(C_m(k)=|\{x:d_m(x)=k\}|\)，\(\Psi(k)\) 为 Weinstein 的层值 \(k\) 生成轨道数；则 \(C_m(1)=2\)，且对 \(k\ge2\)，\(C_m(k)\le4\Psi(k)\)，并在 \(m\ge2k\) 时等号成立。
+若 \(\kappa=-\left.\frac d{ds}\frac{\zeta(s-1)}{\zeta(s)}\right|_{\sigma_0}\)，则 \(S_{-s}(m)=\sum_xd_m(x)^{-s}\to\mathcal Z(s)=4/(2-\zeta(s-1)/\zeta(s))-2\) 当且仅当 \(s>\sigma_0\)；相应层值 Gibbs 律全变差收敛至 \(Q_s(1)=2/\mathcal Z(s),Q_s(k)=4\Psi(k)k^{-s}/\mathcal Z(s)\ (k\ge2)\)，\(\mathcal Z(s)=4/[\kappa(s-\sigma_0)]+O(1)\)，且 \(\mathbb E\log K=(s-\sigma_0)^{-1}+O(1)\)、\({\rm Var}(\log K)=(s-\sigma_0)^{-2}+O(1)\)。
+\(\sum_{k\le x}\Psi(k)\sim x^{\sigma_0}/(\sigma_0\kappa)\)、\(\sum_{k\le x}\Psi(k)k^{-\sigma_0}\sim\kappa^{-1}\log x\)；若 \(\beta_*=\log\varphi/\log(2/\varphi)\)，则 \(t>-\beta_*\) 时 \(\liminf m^{-1}\log S_t(m)>0\)。对每个实 \(t\)，\(P(t)=\lim m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\) 存在，\(t\le-\sigma_0\) 时为 0，\(t>-\sigma_0\) 时为方程 \(\sum_{q\ge2}\sum_{(p,q)=1}q^te^{-P(t)c(p/q)}=1\) 的唯一正解，并在该开区间解析且严格凸。
+临界点 \(t_c=-\sigma_0\) 有 \(P'_-(t_c)=0,P'_+(t_c)=v_c>0\)；且 \(S_{-\sigma_0}(m)\sim4m/\mu_C\)，有限窗临界 Gibbs 律满足 \((m^{-1}\log d_m,m^{-1}\ell_m)\Rightarrow(v_cU,U)\)。
+因此不存在如下全局表示：在包含 \(\mathbb R\) 的连通复邻域上算子范数解析的紧算子族，其每个实参数均有正的、代数单的、孤立且局部一致谱隙的特征值 \(r(t)\)，同时 \(m^{-1}\log S_t(m)\to\log r(t)\)。
+若 \(X_m\) 在合法词集上均匀，则 \(m^{-1}\log d_m(X_m)\) 以速度 \(m\) 满足与上文相同的完全 LDP、有效域及率函数；特别地 \(0<\alpha<v_c\) 的缩小邻域概率指数恰为 \(-I(\alpha)\)。
+对任意固定互异素数集 \(\mathcal P=\{p_i\}_{i=1}^d\)，稿件还证明生成元“素指数--代价”级数为 \(1/(1-A_{\mathcal P}(\mathbf z,u))\)，去掉代价标记后为 \([2-\prod_i(1-z_i)/(1-p_i z_i)]^{-1}\)，并以边界权 \(4,3,1\) 精确转移到 \(C_m(k)\)。
+若 \(\boldsymbol\rho\in\mathbb S_{+}^{d-1}\)、\(\mathbf n_N=N\boldsymbol\rho+O(1)\)，唯一鞍点由 \(\prod_i(1-z_i)/(1-p_i z_i)=2\)、\((p_i-1)z_i/[(1-z_i)(1-p_i z_i)]=\tau\rho_i\) 确定；令 \(m_C=\mathbb EC\)、\(v_{\mathcal P,\rho}=2\tau\sum\rho_i\log p_i/m_C\)。若 \(\log q_{\mathbf n_N}/m_N\to\alpha<v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)，则该精确纤维层最终非空，计数渐近为自由词数的 4 倍，且其均匀律下 \(L(g)/m_N\to\alpha/v_{\mathcal P,\rho}\)（概率收敛；紧方向集及严格非活跃预算上一致）。
+未证明或已撤回的内容：三个具名问题均未解决，即 Weinstein 的 \(F\)-primitive 数刻画、其凸包猜想 9.1、以及 2022 年三类部件数差猜想 3.3；现有工具分别缺少次序敏感最小值比较、全斜率支撑不等式及三次单位根消去。
+“全实轴解析且严格凸的压力”不是未完成目标而是错误命题：已证 \(P=0\) 于整个冻结半轴而 \(P(0)=\log\varphi\)。固定素数支撑在活跃截断 \(j=m+O(1)\) 的锐局部渐近亦未证；二进方向代价已有无穷二阶矩且无正指数矩，所缺为指数条件下的格点稳定/半稳定局部更新定理。
+本文也不声称临界配分和的 \(m^{3-\sigma_0}\) 二阶项、任意薄窗条件极限定理或第二/固定第 \(k\) 大纤维的闭式分类。
+开发早期的有限状态归一化 transducer、碰撞核及其 Perron/递推多项式链条因反例而撤回；主文及补编不再声称 \(\lambda_q\) 的代数性、算术窗递推多项式、精确核证书、Galois 群或 Chebotarev 密度。
+归属边界：外来且已引用的是 Zeckendorf 唯一表示；Kocábová--Masáková--Pelantová 的 \(R\) 极值与全部极大整数；Weinstein 的自由生成元幺半群、乘子乘法性、轨道分解、最终层稳定及 Dirichlet 级数；Kesseböhmer--Stratmann 与 Fiala--Kleban--Özlük 的 Stern--Brocot/Knauf 分母压力。
+外来且已引用的还有 Feller 更新定理、Wiener--Ikehara、Gärtner--Ellis/Dembo--Zeitouni、解析扰动论及 Pemantle--Wilson 光滑极小点理论；Sanna 的整数次矩与零温端点、Kempton 的矩阵余循环动力学、Sarig 的可数状态相变仅作最近比较，均未被宣称为本文结果。
+本文自有部分是显式反转--补映射与仿射置换、分拆差及双区间恒等式、早层一致计数界、带代价更新恒等式及全实压力转移、临界线性常数与均匀共存律、补齐两种奇偶的非暴露斜率下界、二维 LDP，以及有限素数支撑的精确接口与严格非活跃区淬火转移；极值数值及极大整数本身不主张新颖。
+文献审计检索了 arXiv、Crossref、Google Scholar、Semantic Scholar、zbMATH Open 及可见引文链，未定位到上述一层/有限窗全实压力、临界共存或完整 LDP；但 MathSciNet 无订阅访问，部分接口曾返回 429，因此这只是可复核的检索证据，绝非穷尽性优先权证明。
+机器证据：verify_deepening_delta.py 精确枚举 \(m=0,\ldots,28\)（最大系数数组 832040），核验双区间恒等式、闭式、递推及全部极大类，结果为 0 failure、0 counterexample；verify_speed_separation.py 以有理数和定向舍入区间证成 \(v_2-v_c\ge0.001171960512764161\)，STATUS: PASS。
+verify_real_tilt_pressure.py 在 \(m=12,16,20,24,28,32\)、\(t=-8,-7.75,\ldots,10\) 上检查压力/LDP 形状，并完成层界 2--22、9011 项全实层审计、7938 项桥接、3396 项局部谱、210 项实倾斜更新、1815 项轨道补齐等检查，报告 0 numerical failures；两组回归测试合计 20 passed。
+这些有限计算、符号恒等式检查和区间证书仅用于发现反例及复核公式，不替代无限性解析证明。
+优先权问题请置于四项判断之首：我们的一个姊妹稿刚被审稿人发现遗漏的既有结果，致使整篇文章的优先权叙述发生根本改变；请尤其主动追查而不要仅认可现有文献表。
+1. 数学是否正确？请逐一指出任何您无法跟随、认为有缺口或认为错误的陈述及具体证明步骤。
+2. 结果是否新，且上述优先权叙述是否准确？请给出最近的既有工作，判断我们是否正确、充分地归功于它；若某项实为已知推论或标准转移，请明确指出并说明应如何改写归属。

+尊敬的专家：请您以 Transactions of the American Mathematical Society（TAMS）审稿人的标准审阅本稿，而非为我们寻找新选题或提出“升档”构想。
